----- BUILD BRIEF -----
# Build brief: Compression Range Breakout (round 3, last plan round, after the PM's pivot)

OHLCV specialist for the PM. Shared rules: `PM/WORKER-BRIEF.md`,
`PM/CAPABILITIES.md`, `PM/cycle1/ux-standard.md` (binding),
`PM/cycle1/validation-protocol.md`; structure as `brief-rvol-clock.md`.
Reviews: `pm/log/codex/20261026T151836Z-...` (round 1), `20261026T153353Z-...`
(round 2). The PM's rulings and the pivot are listed in the change lists.

## The trading question

"Did price leave an established compression range with activity behind the
break bar, and did it come back inside?" Trend is secondary context: it
colours a border and a table row, it never decides.

## Why a fixed range, not a squeeze (the pivot)

The round-3 scratch comparison showed a frozen 20-bar range qualified by
width (k = 2) matching or beating the Bollinger-inside-Keltner squeeze on
every design metric: hits 20/20 versus 18/20, filtered early events 0 versus
10, level validity 20/20 versus 18/18, outcome fixtures 10/10 versus 9/10,
range-control markers 0.4 versus 10.8 per 1,000. The fixtures are monotone
contractions, which favour a fixed-range qualifier, so this is not a claim
that squeezes are worse on real data. The pivot is justified by simplicity
(one ring fewer, no band arithmetic, one sentence: "price has stayed inside a
range narrower than twice its recent bar range") plus equal or better numbers.
The squeeze qualifier stays as a test-only variant, and the live run and the
v2 clause keep the question open.

## Target user and decision

Intraday and swing breakout traders on BTC, ETH, ES, NQ, AAPL, TSLA. Decision:
act on a close outside the compression range, stand aside once it fails, or
treat it as counter-trend context. Descriptive only: "range+vol" means both
activity tests passed on the break bar; "held outside 5 bars" means the
failure window expired, not that a trade worked.

## Identity

- Folder `apps/web/src/lib/indicators/compression-breakout/`; def
  `compressionBreakout.ts`; pure state machine `compressionBreakoutMath.ts`;
  tests in `tests/`, including the test-only baseline and variant defs.
- id `compressionBreakout`, label `"Compression Breakout (20)"`, category
  `"technical"`, placement `{ kind: "overlay" }`, `maxReplayBars` 10,080.
- `OHLCV_POLICY`: reads `bar.open/high/low/close/volume`, `bar.ts_open`,
  `bar.interval_ms`, `bar.closed` only. Every archived instrument; equities
  fine; protocol check 9 N/A, stated in the doc. No `timeframeGate`.

## Data, validity and storage

**Price valid**: open, high, low, close finite, `high ≥ low`, `low ≤ close ≤
high`. **Volume valid**: finite, `≥ 0`. An invalid-price bar contributes
nothing (no ring entry, no range change, no age, no event; status `No usable
price`). Invalid volume keeps the price context; the bar enters no volume
observation and its volume cannot be tested (`vol n/a`). Missing bars
contribute nothing. **Means span valid observations across gaps.** No library
stream drives an event (round 1 evidence); own committed-bar rings, primitives
from `relative-volume/relativeVolumeMath.ts`:

| Ring | Capacity | Serves | Tail |
|---|---|---|---|
| high, low | `rangeLength` (≤ 50) | range width and edges | provisional for the status row; the range opens at commit |
| true range | `2 × rangeLength` (≤ 100) | reference width (the `rangeLength` valid bars preceding the window); range denominator (the preceding 20 valid observations) | excluded from both |
| volume | 20 | volume denominator | excluded |
| close | `trendSlow` (≤ 400) | both MAs | excluded |
| fast-MA history | 6 | slope | excluded |

**True range** uses the previous committed close; on the first bar and on the
first bar after a gap it is `high − low`, so an overnight jump never enters a
reference or a denominator. The slot-median volume baseline is a v2
`volumeRule` option (`createRelativeVolumeEngine`, `reading.rvol`); v1 uses
the rolling denominators so the grade is testable on 400-bar fixtures.

## Calculation

Settings: `rangeLength` 20, `widthMultiple` k 2, `minCompressionBars` 6,
`confirmMultiple` 1.5, `failBars` 5, `holdBars` 40, `trendFast` 50,
`trendSlow` 200. Constant `RANGE_LIFETIME` 500 committed bars.
`committedIndex` counts committed valid-price bars.

1. **Tail and snapshot.** The first dispatch of a new `ts_open` creates the
   tail record and copies the committed state `S`: the five rings,
   `prevClose`, `lastCommittedTsOpen`, `committedIndex`, the active range
   (`hi, lo, openedAt, age, broken, excursion`), the held ranges with
   remaining hold, `lastEvent`. About 600 numbers once per `ts_open`, never
   per revision. **`S` is never modified**; every evaluation, revision and
   correction works on a copy `W`.
2. **Gap (on `W`).** If `ts_open − W.lastCommittedTsOpen > interval_ms`, drop
   the active range from `W` (held ranges stay); status `Gap · range reset`.
   An invalid-price tail cannot reset the committed range.
3. **Provisional values from `W` plus the tail.** `qualified` = width of the
   last `rangeLength` committed highs and lows (`rangeLength − 1` plus the
   tail, status row only) `≤ k ×` mean true range of the `rangeLength` valid
   bars preceding that window; `widthRatio = width / reference` (`null` if the
   reference is 0 or short). Denominators `avgRange20`, `avgVolume20`: mean of
   the preceding 20 valid observations, available only when 20 exist and the
   mean is `> 0`. `gradeBasis` per component = denominator available **and**
   this bar's value valid: 0 none, 1 range, 2 volume, 3 both testable. Trend:
   `fastMA`, `slowMA` over committed closes; slope = `fastMA` now versus five
   committed bars earlier; `up` if `close > slowMA` and slope `> 0`, `down` on
   the mirror, `flat` otherwise; `unknown` until `trendSlow + 5` committed
   closes, or whenever `trendFast ≥ trendSlow`.
4. **Decision, exactly one per bar, read only against `W.active`.**
   - `W.active.broken`: close inside the frozen `[lo, hi]` → `failed`; else
     `committedIndex − brokenIndex === failBars` → `heldOutside`; else
     `pending`. Window = the `failBars` committed bars after the break bar,
     last included.
   - else `W.active` exists and close `> hi` or `< lo`: if `age ≥
     minCompressionBars` → break up / down, tests on testable components only
     (`trueRange ≥ confirmMultiple × avgRange20`, `volume ≥ confirmMultiple ×
     avgVolume20`), `breakGrade` 3 both passed, 1 range only, 2 volume only,
     0 none; if `age < minCompressionBars` → `abandoned` (the range never
     established; silent, not held). Break beats lifetime on the same bar.
   - else `W.active` exists and `committedIndex − openedAt ≥ RANGE_LIFETIME`
     → `expired`.
   - else `none`.
5. **Commit, first dispatch with `closed === true`, in this order.**
   (a) every held range's countdown decrements; one at 0 is removed and its
   handle freed (`holdBars` 0: a retired range disappears at once);
   (b) valid high, low, close and true range enter their rings, valid volume
   its ring, `fastMA` the slope ring;
   (c) the decision is applied: `break` → `broken = {committedIndex, dir,
   grade, basis}`, `excursion` = this bar's high/low; `failed`, `heldOutside`,
   `expired` → the range moves to the held list (`holdBars` remaining;
   `expired` styled like `heldOutside`), `active = null`, `lastEvent`
   updated; `abandoned` → `active = null`, nothing held, no event; `pending`
   → excursion extended, `age += 1`; `none` with an active range → `age += 1`;
   (d) no active range, the window is ready, `qualified` on the committed
   rings, decision `none` → open `{hi, lo}` = the committed window's extremes,
   `openedAt: committedIndex, age: 0`. **Edges never move after opening**; the
   rolling qualification is not re-checked (a frozen range ends only by
   break, abandonment or lifetime);
   (e) `prevClose`, `lastCommittedTsOpen`, `committedIndex` advance; `W`
   becomes the new `S`. `age` counts committed bars inside the frozen edges.
6. **No widening, no re-arm.** A failed break retires its range; a new range
   must requalify and establish again.
7. **Superseded without close**: nothing written; the provisional marker is
   removed on the next dispatch.
8. **Corrections**: a closed re-dispatch of the tail re-runs 2 to 5 from a
   fresh copy of the untouched `S` (cancelled break, removed failure, grade
   change, invalid-to-valid and back). Older corrections arrive as `REBUILT`.
9. **Outputs** (`readOutput`). Persistent: `rangeHigh`, `rangeLow` (active
   range only, incl. pending; `null` otherwise), `widthRatio`, `qualified`,
   `trend` −1/0/1 or `null`, `rangeAge`, `pending` 0/1 (1 from the bar after
   the break through the outcome bar). Pulses, non-zero on the event bar
   only: `breakDir`, `breakGrade`, `gradeBasis`, `failed`, `heldOutside`.
   `rangeHigh`/`rangeLow` are the line outputs.
10. **Pinned transitions** (seed-7 breakout, break at 220, `holdBars` 40):
    `breakDir = 1`, `breakGrade = 3`, `gradeBasis = 3` at 220 only; `pending =
    1` at 221..225; `heldOutside = 1` at 225; `rangeHigh/rangeLow` non-null
    through 225, `null` from 226 until a new range; the held range is drawn
    226..265, its handle freed at 266's commit before any range opens at 266;
    `lastEvent` reads `↑ 220 · range+vol` from 220's commit. `holdBars` 0:
    nothing drawn at 226. A range opened at `committedIndex` 100 with no break
    expires at 600's decision. A range left at `age` 3 is abandoned silently.
11. **Readiness per component.** Qualification and range creation from
    zero-based index `2 × rangeLength − 1` (39 at default; `qualified` and
    `widthRatio` `null` before); grading from index 20 with no invalid bars
    before; trend from `trendSlow + 5`. First possible break at index `39 +
    minCompressionBars`.
12. **Table strings, ≤ 30 characters.** Row 1 `Range 61240–61480`
    (instrument precision, no thousands separators; if the row would exceed
    30 characters both edges round to six significant digits), `Warming up ·
    12 of 40 bars`, or `No range`. Row 2 `Trend ↑ · MA 50/200`, `Trend ↓ ·
    MA 50/200`, `Trend flat · MA 50/200`, `Trend · warming 120/205`, `Trend ·
    fast ≥ slow`. Row 3, first match: `No usable price`; `Gap · range reset`;
    `Failed break`; `Held outside · 5 bars`; `Range expired · 500 bars`;
    break strings `Break ↑ · range+vol`, `range only`, `vol only`,
    `unconfirmed` (both testable), `range · vol n/a`, `vol n/a` (volume not
    testable; range passed / failed), `vol · range n/a`, `range n/a`, `no
    baseline`; `Pending · 2 of 5 bars`; `Range · 9 bars · 1.6×`; `Range
    forming · 3 of 6`; `No range`. Row 4 `Last · ↑ 12:34 · range+vol` or `—`.
    Visible from bar 0; provisional styling on the forming tail.
13. **No future data; fail closed.** Values at bar t use bars with `ts_open ≤
    t.ts_open`; any non-finite intermediate yields `null`.

## Default view

Overlay. The active range as a `draw.box`: fill always neutral purple
`#8672b3` at 0.12 alpha; the 1 px border takes the trend colour (bull / bear
pair, purple when flat or unknown), so direction is carried only by the
arrows. A forming range (`age < minCompressionBars`) draws at 0.06 alpha with
no border; an established one at 0.12 with the border. A retired range is held
`holdBars` bars at 0.06 alpha: `heldOutside`/`expired` keep the purple border,
`failed` takes `--warn`. Pool of 8 handles (one active, seven held); a ninth
evicts the oldest held. Drawing skipped when `drawNow === false`.
`rangeHigh`/`rangeLow` lines, width 1, purple. Table as rule 12, top right,
four rows. Nothing drawn before readiness except the table.

## Controls

Primary (three): `rangeLength` "Range length (bars)" 20, 10..50;
`minCompressionBars` "Minimum compression (bars)" 6, 2..30; `confirmMultiple`
"Confirm above (×)" 1.5, 1.0..3.0, step 0.1; all recompute. Advanced:
`widthMultiple` "Width limit (× bar range)" 2, 1..4, step 0.5; `failBars` 5
(1..20); `holdBars` 40 (0..200); `trendFast` 50 (10..199); `trendSlow` 200
(20..400). Visibility: `showMarkers`, `showRanges` (true, recompute). No
colour pickers. One hint per control; a `staticHint` naming the readiness
rules. `releaseBars` is removed (see Round 3 pivot, item 7).

## Alert conditions

Two event types. **Break**: `arrowUp` below or `arrowDown` above the bar in
the bull/bear pair, drawn when `breakGrade ≥ 1` (solid at 3, 50 percent alpha
at 1 or 2); grade 0 is table and range only. **Failed break**: `circle`,
`inBar`, `--warn`, only for a marked break. One decision per bar, so at most
one marker per bar; markers are a pure function of the bar's decision.

## Fixtures

Design seeds 7..16, both directions. **Evaluation seeds 17..21 untouched**:
nothing is chosen on them; reported in a separate table.

- `compressionBreakoutBars({ seed, direction })`, harness defaults.
- New generators for `validation/fixtures.ts`, specified before the build.
  `compressionFailedBreakBars`: identical to `compressionBreakoutBars` through
  `breakoutIndex` (same generator stream); bar `breakoutIndex + 1`: `open` =
  previous close, `close = startPrice`, `high = max(open, close) + 5`, `low =
  min(open, close) − 5`, `volume = 1.5 × baseVolume`, `buyShare` 0.5; later
  bars follow the compression generator at progress 1 (half width
  `finalHalfWidth`; draws target, wick, volume, buyShare; `volume = baseVolume
  × (0.5 + 0.2 × random())`). Label `failIndex = breakoutIndex + 1`.
  `compressionContinuationBars`: identical through `breakoutIndex`; later
  bars `open` = previous close, `raw = open + (random() − 0.5) × 16`, `close =
  direction > 0 ? max(edge + 0.2 × initialHalfWidth, raw) : min(edge − 0.2 ×
  initialHalfWidth, raw)` (`edge` the labelled extreme), wick `random() × 8`,
  `volume = baseVolume × (1 + 0.6 × random())`, `buyShare = 0.5 + 0.12 ×
  direction + (random() − 0.5) × 0.08`. Label `heldIndex = breakoutIndex + 5`.
  `longCompressionBars` (adversarial replay): `compressionBreakoutBars` with
  `leadBars` 40, `compressionBars` 700, `count` 1,400; the test rebuilds from
  index 300.
- Correction script on seed 7: bar 220 closed as the break, re-dispatched
  closed with `close = compressionHigh − 1` (event gone, range stays active);
  bar 220 with `volume = NaN` then valid (`vol n/a` to `range+vol`); bar 221
  closed inside then outside (failure removed, `pending`). Each must equal a
  clean feed of the corrected series.
- Controls `trendBars`, `rangeBars` (1,000 bars, seeds 7..11); protocol edge
  cases on seed 7; `relativeVolumeCryptoFixture()` for `probeReplayPooled`.

Scoring, identical for every def: **matched** = event index in
`[breakoutIndex, breakoutIndex + 2]`; **hit** = first matched event with the
labelled direction; **wrong-direction matched** = first matched event against
it; **early** = before `breakoutIndex` (lead / compression split);
**unmatched** = after the window; **missed** = no matched event; **level
valid** = on a hit, `hi ≤ compressionHigh`, `lo ≥ compressionLow`, close
beyond the tested edge. Reported for all events and under the identical
range-or-volume filter (`trueRange ≥ 1.5 × avgRange20` or `volume ≥ 1.5 ×
avgVolume20`). Outcome fixtures score the labelled failure or hold.

## Baselines and variant (test-only defs with their own code)

1. **Squeeze-qualified variant** (`squeezeQualifiedVariant`): the round-2
   machine, qualified by Bollinger (window mean, population standard
   deviation, ×2) inside a Keltner-style channel (same mean, ×1.5 mean true
   range) over own finite-window rings, `minSqueezeBars` 6, `releaseBars` 3;
   otherwise identical grades, outcomes, lifetime and filter.
2. **TTM release** (`ta.ttmSqueeze(20, 2, 20, 1.5)`): `isSqueezed` turns
   false after ≥ 6 true bars; direction = sign of `momentum`.
3. **Bollinger crossing** (`ta.bollinger(20, 2)`): first close outside a band
   after an inside close.

All three run on the design seeds, the evaluation seeds and the live run,
side by side with the product. **v2 clause**: if the live run or the
evaluation seeds show the squeeze variant catching coils the range misses
(hits or level validity), v2 adds `qualifier: range | squeeze` under Advanced;
otherwise it stays out.

## Acceptance

Scratch replication (`sim3lib.mjs`, `sim4.mjs`, `sim5.mjs`, specialist
scratchpad) over 20 seed-directions; **provisional until reproduced through
the runtime**:

| Def | Hits | Wrong dir | Early all (lead) | Early filtered | Level valid | Failure fixture | Hold fixture | Range control marked/1,000 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Product (range, k 2, min 6) | 20/20 | 0 | 4 (0) | 0 | 20/20 | 10/10 | 10/10 | 0 |
| Squeeze variant | 18/20 | 0 | 60 (42) | 10 | 18/18 | 9/10 | 9/10 | 10.8 |
| TTM release | 18/20 | 0 | 32 (12) | 0 | n/a | none | none | — |
| Bollinger crossing | 20/20 | 0 | 118 (82) | 38 | n/a | none | none | — |

Trend control 0 for all; unmatched: Bollinger 2, others 0; every product hit
is grade 3. Values tried for k: 2, 2.5, 3, 4 (all-events early 8, 8, 28, 66
without the age gate; filtered 0, 0, 0, 24; hits 20/20 at every k); k 2 is
the strictest value that qualifies every design coil. With the age gate the
product's early events fall to 4 and its range-control markers to 0. The
rolling re-check variant (`releaseBars` 3) was tried and rejected: 16/20 hits,
8/10 on both outcome fixtures, seed 7 without any range.

Bounds, set from the frozen-range numbers with margin: hits ≥ 18/20 with level
valid on every hit; failure fixture ≥ 9/10; hold fixture ≥ 9/10; filtered
early ≤ 4; range control ≤ 5 marked per 1,000; trend 0; no wrong-direction
events; `maxPerBar` 1. The doc page states the design-seed numbers for all
four defs, and the evaluation seeds 17..21 in full. Live observation (REST
transport, `data-api.binance.vision`): 10,080-bar BTC-USDT and ETH-USDT
snapshots plus 30 minutes of forming revisions and closes, driven with
`driveLive` and compared with `replayCapture`: equal outputs and confirmed
markers, no fault, ≤ 40 markers per 1,000 on 1m and resampled 5m; the four
event lists quoted side by side in the doc. Density, honesty and replay only.

## Replay policy

(a) **Bounded lifetime.** No range lives past `RANGE_LIFETIME` 500 committed
bars, so every state has a finite horizon: `max(trendSlow + 5, 500 + holdBars
+ failBars)` retained bars (545 at defaults). After the horizon, live and a
replay from the first retained bar must be exactly equal in every output,
event, marker and drawn range; the REBUILT equivalence test asserts a non-zero
compared count after it.
(b) **Pre-horizon difference, documented and tested.** On
`longCompressionBars` with a REBUILT from index 300, the live instance's range
(opened once the window qualifies, expired 500 bars later, a new range after)
and the rebuilt instance's range (opened once its own 40-bar window qualifies)
differ in `openedAt`, `rangeAge`, `rangeHigh`/`rangeLow` and the drawn range
until the horizon; the test pins the expected difference bar by bar and checks
that both see the break at 740 with the same grade. The doc says that after a
reload a range older than the retained history is seen only from the first
retained bar.

## Success criteria

1. Hand-computed values on a 50-bar fixture: reference width, qualification,
   readiness per component, frozen edges, read-before-write test, grade and
   basis with pinned denominators, failure, hold, abandonment, expiry, held
   countdown with `holdBars` 40 and 0, pool eviction, `trendFast ≥ trendSlow`.
2. Protocol checks 1 to 7 and 10 PASS, 9 N/A: truncation invariance on
   breakout and trend fixtures; live-versus-replay equal at zero tolerance;
   duplicate, REBUILT, time gap (`firstBarAfterGapIndex` shows `Gap · range
   reset`, its true range is `high − low`, nothing earlier changes), forming
   tail, the correction script, the invalid-price tail leaving `S` intact.
3. Pooled p99 `onBar + readOutput` under 0.05 ms over bars 1..10,080 with the
   snapshot copy in the timed region; rollover with 10,080 outputs, no markers
   before retention, heap slope within 3× the harness floor, 8 handles.
4. UX: three primary controls; strings as rule 12; screenshots at two zooms:
   forming range, established range, confirmed break, failed break, held
   outside, warm-up, dialog with Advanced closed and open, cream and dark.
5. Doc page and README row; `validate-docs`, web suite, `tsc`, lint green;
   Codex code review APPROVED; validator ACCEPT.

## Limitations to state

Descriptive, not predictive. "Held outside 5 bars" means the failure window
expired, nothing about profit; a return inside is failure of this breakout
rule, not evidence for fading. Readiness: range at `2 × rangeLength` bars,
grading at 20 valid bars, trend at `trendSlow + 5`. The qualifier is a
fixed-length range, not a volatility squeeze; the fixtures favour it and the
live run is where the two are compared. A failed break retires its range; a
second break needs a fresh range. Ranges expire at 500 bars. Equity overnight
gaps reset the range and their first true range is `high − low`. A range older
than the retained history is seen only from the first retained bar after a
reload. Unit-free.

## Deferred

`qualifier: range | squeeze` (v2 clause); re-arm and widening; time-of-day
volume rule; swing pivots; break-bar delta grade; session-open rule.

## Seams for L1 and L2

`rangeHigh`, `rangeLow`, `rangeAge`, `pending`, `breakDir`, `breakGrade`,
`gradeBasis`, `failed`, `heldOutside`: the L1 tape tally grades intensity on
the `breakDir` bar; the L2 Break Defense Monitor watches liquidity at the
edges while `rangeAge ≥ minCompressionBars`. Shared through
`compressionBreakoutMath.ts`.

## Budget

One build run, UX before the end, at most two fix rounds after Codex code
review and validation; a third failure returns the concept to the backlog.

## Ten-line summary

1. A committed 20-bar high/low range qualifies when narrower than twice the mean true range of the 20 bars before it; edges freeze at once.
2. Six committed bars inside make it established; leaving earlier abandons it silently.
3. Break = close outside the frozen edges; break beats lifetime.
4. Grade = true range and volume versus the preceding 20 valid observations; basis says what was testable; `vol n/a` is distinct from a failed test.
5. Failure = back inside within 5 bars; "held outside 5 bars" otherwise; both retire the range.
6. No re-arm; ranges expire at 500 bars; a gap resets the range and its first true range is `high − low`.
7. Neutral purple range; trend colours only the border and row 2; arrows carry direction.
8. Snapshot never modified; corrections re-run from it; pulses on the event bar, `pending` persists.
9. Exact replay equality after a 545-bar horizon; the pre-horizon difference is pinned on `longCompressionBars`.
10. Squeeze variant, TTM release and Bollinger crossing reported beside the product on design seeds, evaluation seeds and the live run; v2 clause for the qualifier.

## Effort estimate

M: five small rings, one state machine, the snapshot copy, three test-only
defs and the adversarial replay test; UX half a day; one validation loop
expected.

## Plan-gate history

Round 1 REVISE: library streams change box creation; freeze tested edges;
transition priority; rollback; denominator validity; table edges and basis;
fair baselines; outcome fixtures; evaluation seeds; `maxReplayBars`. Round 2
REVISE: outcome wording; storage versus `squeezeLength`; trend settings;
implicit transitions; snapshot mutability; readiness; `bar.open`; `vol n/a`;
post-gap true range; colour semantics; unbounded state versus replay; an
independent frozen-range baseline; wrong-direction category.

## Round 2 changes

Own-state finite-window bands; frozen tested edges; no re-arm; whole-state
snapshot with restore-and-reapply; validity rules and `gradeBasis`; table
edges and test-basis strings; all-events and filtered comparison with
direction-correct hits; two frozen generators and a correction script;
`maxReplayBars` 10,080; deferred list.

## Round 3 changes (rulings 1 to 10)

inclusion stated; `trendFast`/`trendSlow` as set with readiness and the

## Round 3 pivot (PM decision on the round-3 finding)

1. Product renamed Compression Range Breakout: folder, id, label and math
   module as ruled; qualifier = committed `rangeLength` high/low range with
   width ≤ k × mean true range of the preceding `rangeLength` valid bars, k 2
   under Advanced; edges frozen at qualification; `minCompressionBars` 6
   counts committed bars inside the frozen edges (ruling 1).
2. Primary controls `rangeLength`, `minCompressionBars`, `confirmMultiple`;
   Advanced `widthMultiple`, `failBars`, `holdBars`, `trendFast`, `trendSlow`;
   `bbMult`/`kcMult` removed (ruling 2).
3. Bollinger-inside-Keltner kept as `squeezeQualifiedVariant`, a test-only def
   reported beside the product everywhere, with the v2 `qualifier` clause
   (ruling 3).
4. Bounds set from the frozen-range numbers with margin; the doc states the
   design-seed numbers for all four defs (ruling 4).
5. Rationale paragraph added (ruling 5). 6. Effort M (ruling 6).
7. **Deviation flagged for the PM**: `releaseBars` is removed rather than
   kept under Advanced. Its squeeze meaning (tolerating a flickering band
   condition) has no counterpart once edges are frozen; the only reading
   available, re-checking the rolling qualification and discarding after
   `releaseBars` misses, was simulated and drops the product to 16/20 hits and
   8/10 on both outcome fixtures (seed 7 never establishes a range), while a
   frozen range that ends only by break, abandonment or lifetime gives the
   numbers in Acceptance. Fixture `longSqueezeBars` renamed
   `longCompressionBars`; the replay horizon is unchanged at 545.

## PM note before Codex round 3

Pivot accepted as rewritten. `releaseBars` removed: a frozen range ends only
by break, abandonment or lifetime, and the simulated alternative meaning was
harmful (16/20 hits). File renamed from `brief-squeeze-breakout.md`; the

## PM approval after Codex round 3 (the last plan round)

APPROVED by PM authority. Codex round 3 (pm/log/codex/20261026T154840Z-plan-brief-compression-breakout.last.md)
returned REVISE; Codex is right that authority does not resolve technical
contradictions, so each point below is accepted as a binding amendment that
replaces the conflicting sentence in the body. The builder writes the final
rules into the doc page and the tests.

1. **Replay policy, truthful.** A 500-bar lifetime does not synchronise the
   range phase of a live and a rebuilt instance on a continuously qualifying
   market. Policy: outputs, events, markers and drawn ranges are required
   equal from the first retained bar at which the live instance holds no
   active and no held range (a "quiet bar") and the 20 valid denominators
   plus trendSlow + 5 closes lie inside retention; a non-zero compared count
   is asserted on the breakout and control fixtures. Before the quiet bar the
   difference is documented and pinned on a constant-width, non-zero true
   range fixture of 3,000 bars rebuilt at offsets 0, 137 and 500. Lifetime,
   failBars and holdBars count committed dispatched bars (invalid-price bars
   included in the count; they skip every other commit step); denominators
   count valid observations and carry no bar bound.
2. **Establishment timeline.** The range opens on the qualifying committed
   bar with age 0; age increments on each later committed bar whose close
   stays inside; eligible for a break when age ≥ minCompressionBars, so the
   earliest break at defaults is index 46 (open at 39). Fixtures, readiness
   and the doc use this timeline.
3. **Per-bar output snapshot.** readOutput reads a snapshot taken after the
   decision and before retirement, so rangeHigh/rangeLow stay non-null on the
   outcome bar; `pending` is 1 from the bar after the break through the
   outcome bar inclusive and 0 after. The pinned seed-7 example is corrected
   to this timeline.
4. **State names.** `P` = pre-tail committed state kept for corrections of
   the tail; `W` = working copy for every evaluation; `C` = committed state
   after the tail, which seeds the next ts_open. Corrections restore from `P`
   and recompute `C`.
5. **Failure window.** A close beyond the opposite frozen edge during the
   failure window is a failure (it passed back through the range); a close
   outside on the break side stays pending.
6. **Gaps and equities.** The gap rule applies only when interval_ms is below
   one day; daily and weekly bars never reset on calendar gaps. Intraday
   equity ranges reset at the overnight gap, stated as a limitation (the
   session-open rule is deferred). Test on the harness equity fixtures at
   1-minute and daily intervals. Price validity adds low ≤ open ≤ high.
7. **UX.** Control label "Activity threshold (×)" with a hint that one
   component passing gives a faded arrow and both a solid one; lastEvent is
   the latest event of any kind (break, failed, held outside); warm-up shows
   the single row required by the UX standard.
8. **Validation.** Design seeds 7..16 are regression evidence. Evaluation
   seeds 17..21, both directions, frozen criteria before any run: hits ≥ 9 of
   10 with level valid on every hit, filtered early ≤ 2 in total, no
   wrong-direction event in any matched window, failure and hold fixtures on
   those seeds ≥ 4 of 5 each. TTM release and Bollinger crossing are timing
   and density comparators only. Add a plain frozen-range baseline with no
   width qualifier and identical establishment, grading and outcome rules; if
   it matches the product on every metric including range-control density,
   the PM decides at validation whether the width qualifier ships.
   Wrong-direction is defined as a break event opposing the label inside the
   matched window; opposite-direction events elsewhere are reported as early
   or off-label. Expiry and trend readiness are tested with reduced settings
   on separate fixtures. The live run's equality requirement follows the
   policy in amendment 1.

+/**
+ * A wide range that contracts bar by bar over `compressionBars`, then a bar
+ * that closes well outside the compressed range and a trend that follows.
+ * `breakoutIndex` is the bar to detect; anything earlier is a false positive
+ * and anything later is late.
+ */
+export function compressionBreakoutBars(options: CompressionSeriesOptions = {}): LabelledBars<CompressionBreakoutLabels> {
+  return compressionSeries(options, "trend");
+}
+
+export interface CompressionFailedBreakLabels extends CompressionBreakoutLabels {
+  /** The bar after the break that closes back at the middle of the range. */
+  failIndex: number;
+}
+
+/**
+ * `compressionBreakoutBars` through the break bar (identical random stream), then
+ * a bar closing back at `startPrice` on 1.5× base volume, then compression bars
+ * at progress 1 on half volume: the labelled failed break.
+ */
+export function compressionFailedBreakBars(options: CompressionSeriesOptions = {}): LabelledBars<CompressionFailedBreakLabels> {
+  const { bars, labels } = compressionSeries(options, "fail");
+  return { bars, labels: { ...labels, failIndex: labels.breakoutIndex + 1 } };
+}
+
+export interface CompressionContinuationLabels extends CompressionBreakoutLabels {
+  /** Five bars after the break, every close still beyond the edge: the labelled "held outside". */
+  heldIndex: number;
+  heldBars: number;
+}
+
+/**
+ * `compressionBreakoutBars` through the break bar (identical random stream), then
+ * closes held at least a fifth of the initial half width beyond the broken edge
+ * with no trend: the labelled continuation.
+ */
+export function compressionContinuationBars(options: CompressionSeriesOptions & { heldBars?: number } = {}): LabelledBars<CompressionContinuationLabels> {
+  const heldBars = options.heldBars ?? 5;
+  const { bars, labels } = compressionSeries(options, "hold");
+  return { bars, labels: { ...labels, heldIndex: labels.breakoutIndex + heldBars, heldBars } };
+}
+
+export interface LongCompressionLabels extends CompressionBreakoutLabels {
+  /** Where the adversarial replay test rebuilds from, as if the earlier bars had left retention. */
+  rebuildFromIndex: number;
+}
+
+/** A coil that outlives a 500-bar range lifetime: 40 lead bars, 700 compression bars, the break at 740, 1,400 bars in all. */
+export function longCompressionBars(options: CompressionSeriesOptions & { rebuildFromIndex?: number } = {}): LabelledBars<LongCompressionLabels> {
+  const { bars, labels } = compressionSeries({ leadBars: 40, compressionBars: 700, count: 1_400, ...options }, "trend");
+  return { bars, labels: { ...labels, rebuildFromIndex: options.rebuildFromIndex ?? 300 } };
+}
+
+export interface ConstantWidthLabels {
+  regime: "constantWidth";
+  /** Every 20-bar window spans exactly this. */
+  width: number;
+  /** Every bar's true range, so width over the preceding mean true range is `width / trueRange`. */
+  trueRange: number;
+  /** Rebuild offsets the replay-policy test uses to pin the phase difference. */
+  rebuildOffsets: readonly number[];
+}
+
+/**
+ * The replay-policy fixture of brief amendment 1: a constant-width band with a
+ * non-zero true range on every bar and no close ever beyond the band's
+ * extremes, so a frozen range qualifies at every window, never breaks and
+ * expires at the lifetime; a live instance and one rebuilt from an offset then
+ * open their ranges at different phases for as long as the series lasts.
+ * Bars alternate between a down bar closing at `center − amplitude` and an up
+ * bar closing at `center + amplitude`, wicks of 5 beyond the body.
+ */
+export function constantWidthBars(
+  options: FixtureOptions & { amplitude?: number; rebuildOffsets?: readonly number[] } = {},
+): LabelledBars<ConstantWidthLabels> {
+  const { count, startTs, intervalMs, startPrice, identity, baseVolume } = defaults({ count: 3_000, ...options });
+  const amplitude = options.amplitude ?? 50;
+  const wick = 5;
+  const bars: BarFrame[] = [];
+  for (let index = 0; index < count; index += 1) {
+    const up = index % 2 === 1;
+    const open = up ? startPrice - amplitude : startPrice + amplitude;
+    const close = up ? startPrice + amplitude : startPrice - amplitude;
+    bars.push(barFrame(startTs + index * intervalMs, candle(open, close, wick, baseVolume, 0.5), identity, intervalMs));
+  }
+  return {
+    bars,
+    labels: {
+      regime: "constantWidth",
+      width: 2 * amplitude + 2 * wick,
+      trueRange: 2 * amplitude + 2 * wick,
+      rebuildOffsets: options.rebuildOffsets ?? [0, 137, 500],
+    },
+  };
+}

+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { DAY_MS } from "../../../indicators/compression-breakout/compressionBreakoutMath";
+import {
+  compressionBreakoutBars,
+  compressionContinuationBars,
+  compressionFailedBreakBars,
+  constantWidthBars,
+  longCompressionBars,
+} from "../fixtures";
+
+/**
+ * Proving tests for the compression fixture family of the Compression Breakout
+ * brief: the three aftermaths share one generator stream through the break
+ * bar, the labelled outcomes are what the generators promise, and the
+ * constant-width replay fixture has the properties amendment 1 relies on.
+ */
+describe("compression fixtures", () => {
+  it("are deterministic by seed and differ between seeds", () => {
+    expect(compressionBreakoutBars({ seed: 7 }).bars).toEqual(compressionBreakoutBars({ seed: 7 }).bars);
+    expect(compressionBreakoutBars({ seed: 7 }).bars[50]).not.toEqual(compressionBreakoutBars({ seed: 8 }).bars[50]);
+  });
+
+  it("the failed-break and continuation fixtures are identical to the breakout fixture through the break bar", () => {
+    for (const seed of [7, 9, 16]) {
+      const breakout = compressionBreakoutBars({ seed });
+      const failed = compressionFailedBreakBars({ seed });
+      const held = compressionContinuationBars({ seed });
+      const b = breakout.labels.breakoutIndex;
+      expect(failed.bars.slice(0, b + 1)).toEqual(breakout.bars.slice(0, b + 1));
+      expect(held.bars.slice(0, b + 1)).toEqual(breakout.bars.slice(0, b + 1));
+      expect(failed.labels).toMatchObject({ ...breakout.labels, failIndex: b + 1 });
+      expect(held.labels).toMatchObject({ ...breakout.labels, heldIndex: b + 5, heldBars: 5 });
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("the failed break closes back at the start price on 1.5× volume and the compression resumes on reduced volume", () => {
+    const { bars, labels } = compressionFailedBreakBars({ seed: 9, baseVolume: 100, startPrice: 60_000 });
+    const fail = bars[labels.failIndex];
+    expect(fail.open).toBe(bars[labels.breakoutIndex].close);
+    expect(fail.close).toBe(60_000);
+    expect(fail.volume).toBe(150);
+    expect(fail.high).toBe(Math.max(fail.open, fail.close) + 5);
+    for (const bar of bars.slice(labels.failIndex + 1)) {
+      expect(bar.volume).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(50);
+      expect(bar.volume).toBeLessThanOrEqual(70);
+      expect(bar.high).toBeLessThanOrEqual(60_000 + 30 + 1e-9);
+      expect(bar.low).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(60_000 - 30 - 1e-9);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("the continuation keeps every later close a fifth of the initial half width beyond the broken edge, in both directions", () => {
+    for (const direction of [1, -1] as const) {
+      const { bars, labels } = compressionContinuationBars({ seed: 12, direction });
+      const edge = direction > 0 ? labels.compressionHigh : labels.compressionLow;
+      for (const bar of bars.slice(labels.breakoutIndex + 1)) {
+        if (direction > 0) expect(bar.close).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(edge + 60 - 0.01);
+        else expect(bar.close).toBeLessThanOrEqual(edge - 60 + 0.01);
+      }
+      expect(bars[labels.heldIndex].ts_open).toBe(bars[labels.breakoutIndex].ts_open + 5 * 60_000);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("the long compression coil outlives a 500-bar lifetime and rebuilds from 300 by default", () => {
+    const { bars, labels } = longCompressionBars();
+    expect(bars).toHaveLength(1_400);
+    expect(labels.compressionStart).toBe(40);
+    expect(labels.breakoutIndex).toBe(740);
+    expect(labels.rebuildFromIndex).toBe(300);
+    expect(bars[740].close).toBeGreaterThan(labels.compressionHigh);
+  });
+
+  it("the constant-width fixture: every bar's true range is 110, every 20-bar window spans 110, no close leaves the band", () => {
+    const { bars, labels } = constantWidthBars();
+    expect(bars).toHaveLength(3_000);
+    expect(labels).toMatchObject({ width: 110, trueRange: 110, rebuildOffsets: [0, 137, 500] });
+    for (let index = 1; index < bars.length; index += 1) {
+      const bar = bars[index];
+      const prev = bars[index - 1].close;
+      const tr = Math.max(bar.high - bar.low, Math.abs(bar.high - prev), Math.abs(bar.low - prev));
+      expect(tr).toBe(110);
+      expect(bar.close).toBeLessThanOrEqual(60_055);
+      expect(bar.close).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(59_945);
+    }
+    for (let start = 0; start + 20 <= bars.length; start += 97) {
+      const window = bars.slice(start, start + 20);
+      expect(Math.max(...window.map((bar) => bar.high)) - Math.min(...window.map((bar) => bar.low))).toBe(110);
+    }
+    const daily = constantWidthBars({ count: 50, intervalMs: DAY_MS });
+    expect(daily.bars[1].ts_open - daily.bars[0].ts_open).toBe(DAY_MS);
+  });
+});

+import { draw } from "../../chart/draw";
+import type { BoxHandle } from "../../chart/draw/box";
+import type { TableHandle } from "../../chart/draw/table";
+import type { IndicatorBarCtx, LineOutputSpec, RuntimeIndicatorDef, SettingsFieldSchema } from "../../chart/indicatorTypes";
+import {
+  MAX_HELD_RANGES,
+  RANGE_LIFETIME,
+  createCompressionBreakoutEngine,
+  type CompressionBreakoutEngine,
+  type HeldRange,
+  type Reading,
+} from "./compressionBreakoutMath";
+
+/**
+ * Compression Breakout (20): a committed 20-bar high/low range that is
+ * narrower than twice the mean true range of the 20 bars before it, drawn as
+ * a neutral purple box with frozen edges; a break is a close outside it after
+ * six bars inside, graded by the break bar's true range and volume against the
+ * preceding 20 bars (arrow solid when both pass, faded when one does); a close
+ * back inside within five bars is a failed break (circle); otherwise the break
+ * is "held outside 5 bars". Trend (MA 50/200) colours the border and one table
+ * row only. Candle-only, `OHLCV_POLICY`.
+ */
+
+export interface CompressionBreakoutSettings {
+  rangeLength: number;
+  minCompressionBars: number;
+  confirmMultiple: number;
+  widthMultiple: number;
+  failBars: number;
+  holdBars: number;
+  trendFast: number;
+  trendSlow: number;
+  showMarkers: boolean;
+  showRanges: boolean;
+}
+
+export const COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS: CompressionBreakoutSettings = Object.freeze({
+  rangeLength: 20,
+  minCompressionBars: 6,
+  confirmMultiple: 1.5,
+  widthMultiple: 2,
+  failBars: 5,
+  holdBars: 40,
+  trendFast: 50,
+  trendSlow: 200,
+  showMarkers: true,
+  showRanges: true,
+});
+
+/** Fixed for this version: a colour picker would force a replay over the retained bars. */
+export const CB_RANGE_COLOR = "#8672b3";
+export const CB_BULL_COLOR = "#26a69a";
+export const CB_BEAR_COLOR = "#ef5350";
+export const CB_WARN_COLOR = "#b07a1e";
+export const CB_TABLE_TEXT_COLOR = "#d1d4dc";
+export const CB_UNAVAILABLE = "—";
+export const CB_PROVISIONAL_ALPHA = 0.5;
+/** A break with one activity test passed: faded, but still visible on the cream chart at the zoomed-out level (UX finding 11). */
+export const CB_FADED_MARKER_ALPHA = 0.65;
+export const CB_FILL_ESTABLISHED_ALPHA = 0.14;
+export const CB_FILL_FORMING_ALPHA = 0.08;
+export const CB_FILL_HELD_ALPHA = 0.06;
+export const CB_FORMING_BORDER_ALPHA = 0.35;
+export const CB_HELD_BORDER_ALPHA = 0.5;
+export const CB_BOX_POOL = 1 + MAX_HELD_RANGES;
+export const CB_TABLE_MAX_CHARS = 30;
+/** The invisible colour of a range's last edge point; distinct from the hidden-box colour so the two are told apart. */
+export const CB_EDGE_JOIN_COLOR = "rgba(1,1,1,0)";
+const TRANSPARENT = "rgba(0,0,0,0)";
+
+export interface CompressionBreakoutState {
+  engine: CompressionBreakoutEngine;
+  table: TableHandle;
+  /** Handle 0 is the active range; 1..7 the held ranges, oldest first. */
+  boxes: BoxHandle[];
+  reading: Reading | null;
+}
+
+/** `#rgb` or `#rrggbb` with an alpha; any other colour string is returned unchanged. */
+export function withAlpha(color: string, alpha: number): string {
+  const hex = /^#([0-9a-f]{3}|[0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(color.trim());
+  if (hex === null) return color;
+  let digits = hex[1];
+  if (digits.length === 3) digits = digits.split("").map((d) => d + d).join("");
+  const r = Number.parseInt(digits.slice(0, 2), 16);
+  const g = Number.parseInt(digits.slice(2, 4), 16);
+  const b = Number.parseInt(digits.slice(4, 6), 16);
+  return `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${alpha})`;
+}
+
+export function trendColor(trend: -1 | 0 | 1 | null): string {
+  if (trend === 1) return CB_BULL_COLOR;
+  if (trend === -1) return CB_BEAR_COLOR;
+  return CB_RANGE_COLOR;
+}
+
+/** Decimal places a price carries, from its shortest round-trip text; at most eight. */
+export function decimalsOf(value: number): number {
+  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return 0;
+  const text = String(value);
+  const exponent = text.indexOf("e");
+  if (exponent !== -1) return Math.min(8, Math.max(0, -Number.parseInt(text.slice(exponent + 1), 10)));
+  const point = text.indexOf(".");
+  return point === -1 ? 0 : Math.min(8, text.length - point - 1);
+}
+
+function formatAt(value: number, decimals: number): string {
+  return value.toFixed(decimals);
+}
+
+/** Six significant digits with trailing zeros removed, for a row that would not fit. */
+function formatSignificant(value: number): string {
+  const text = Number(value.toPrecision(6)).toString();
+  return text.includes("e") ? value.toPrecision(6) : text;
+}
+
+/**
+ * `Range lo–hi` at the instrument's precision with no thousands separators; if
+ * the row would exceed 30 characters, both edges round to six significant
+ * digits (brief ruling 8).
+ */
+export function rangeRowText(hi: number, lo: number): string {
+  const decimals = Math.max(decimalsOf(hi), decimalsOf(lo));
+  const exact = `Range ${formatAt(lo, decimals)}–${formatAt(hi, decimals)}`;
+  if (exact.length <= CB_TABLE_MAX_CHARS) return exact;
+  return `Range ${formatSignificant(lo)}–${formatSignificant(hi)}`;
+}
+
+function arrow(dir: number): string {
+  return dir > 0 ? "↑" : "↓";
+}
+
+/** The test-basis wording of a break: what passed, and what could not be tested. */
+export function breakBasisText(grade: number, basis: number): string {
+  const rangeTestable = (basis & 1) === 1;
+  const volumeTestable = (basis & 2) === 2;
+  const rangePassed = grade === 1 || grade === 3;
+  const volumePassed = grade === 2 || grade === 3;
+  if (rangeTestable && volumeTestable) {
+    if (rangePassed && volumePassed) return "range+vol";
+    if (rangePassed) return "range only";
+    if (volumePassed) return "vol only";
+    return "unconfirmed";
+  }
+  if (rangeTestable) return rangePassed ? "range, vol n/a" : "vol n/a";
+  if (volumeTestable) return volumePassed ? "vol, range n/a" : "range n/a";
+  return "no baseline";
+}
+
+function timeText(tsOpen: number, intervalMs: number): string {
+  const date = new Date(tsOpen);
+  if (intervalMs >= 86_400_000) return `${date.getUTCDate()} ${["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"][date.getUTCMonth()]}`;
+  return `${String(date.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(date.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
+}
+
+export interface TableRow {
+  text: string;
+  color: string;
+}
+
+/** Row 3 of the table: the state, first match wins (brief rule 12 and amendments 1, 5 and 7). */
+export function stateText(reading: Reading, settings: CompressionBreakoutSettings): string {
+  const d = reading.decision;
+  if (d.kind === "invalidPrice") return "No usable price";
+  if (reading.gap) return "Gap · range reset";
+  if (d.kind === "failed") return "Failed break";
+  if (d.kind === "heldOutside") return `Held outside · ${settings.failBars} bars`;
+  if (d.kind === "expired") return `Range expired · ${RANGE_LIFETIME} bars`;
+  if (d.kind === "break") return `Break ${arrow(d.dir)} · ${breakBasisText(d.grade, d.basis)}`;
+  const active = reading.active;
+  if (d.kind === "pending") return `Holding · ${Math.min(settings.failBars, reading.pendingBars)} of ${settings.failBars} bars`;
+  if (active !== null) {
+    if (active.age >= settings.minCompressionBars) {
+      const ratio = active.widthRatio === null ? "" : ` · width ${active.widthRatio.toFixed(1)}×`;
+      return `Range · ${active.age} bars${ratio}`;
+    }
+    return `Range forming · ${active.age} of ${settings.minCompressionBars}`;
+  }
+  // No range: how far the rolling window is from the width limit, so the row carries a reason.
+  return reading.widthRatio === null ? "Watching" : `Watching · width ${reading.widthRatio.toFixed(1)}×`;
+}
+
+export function trendText(reading: Reading, settings: CompressionBreakoutSettings): string {
+  const pair = `MA ${settings.trendFast}/${settings.trendSlow}`;
+  if (reading.trendStatus === "fastGeSlow") return "Trend off · fast ≥ slow";
+  if (reading.trendStatus === "warming") return `Trend warming · ${reading.closesCount} of ${settings.trendSlow + 5}`;
+  if (reading.trend === 1) return `Trend ↑ · ${pair}`;
+  if (reading.trend === -1) return `Trend ↓ · ${pair}`;
+  return `Trend flat · ${pair}`;
+}
+
+export function lastEventText(reading: Reading, settings: CompressionBreakoutSettings): string {
+  const event = reading.lastEvent;
+  if (event === null) return CB_UNAVAILABLE;
+  const when = timeText(event.tsOpen, reading.intervalMs);
+  switch (event.kind) {
+    case "break":
+      return `Last ${arrow(event.dir)} ${when} · ${breakBasisText(event.grade, event.basis)}`;
+    case "failed":
+      return `Last failed ${arrow(event.dir)} ${when}`;
+    case "heldOutside":
+      return `Last held ${arrow(event.dir)} ${when} · ${settings.failBars} bars`;
+    default:
+      return `Last expired ${when}`;
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * The table rows: during warm-up only `[{ text }]` (one row is written, so one
+ * row shows); afterwards the four rows at full colour. Provisional styling
+ * stays on markers and on the edge lines' last point only: the table is
+ * flushed after every forming revision, so a dimmed forming row would read
+ * as a permanently dimmed table (UX finding 1).
+ */
+export function tableRows(reading: Reading | null, settings: CompressionBreakoutSettings): TableRow[] {
+  const neutral = CB_TABLE_TEXT_COLOR;
+  if (reading === null) return [{ text: `Warming up · 0 of ${2 * settings.rangeLength} bars`, color: neutral }];
+  if (!reading.rangeReady) return [{ text: `Warming up · ${reading.warmupCount} of ${reading.warmupNeeded} bars`, color: neutral }];
+  const row1 = reading.active !== null ? rangeRowText(reading.active.hi, reading.active.lo) : "No range";
+  const d = reading.decision;
+  const stateColor =
+    d.kind === "break" ? (d.dir > 0 ? CB_BULL_COLOR : CB_BEAR_COLOR) : d.kind === "failed" ? CB_WARN_COLOR : neutral;
+  return [
+    { text: row1, color: neutral },
+    { text: trendText(reading, settings), color: trendColor(reading.trend) },
+    { text: stateText(reading, settings), color: stateColor },
+    { text: lastEventText(reading, settings), color: neutral },
+  ];
+}
+
+function hideBox(box: BoxHandle): void {
+  box.setBackgroundColor(TRANSPARENT);
+  box.setBorderColor(TRANSPARENT);
+}
+
+function drawActive(box: BoxHandle, reading: Reading, settings: CompressionBreakoutSettings): void {
+  const active = reading.active;
+  if (active === null) {
+    hideBox(box);
+    return;
+  }
+  const established = active.age >= settings.minCompressionBars;
+  box.setLeftTop(active.openedTs, active.hi);
+  box.setRightBottom(reading.tsOpen, active.lo);
+  box.setBackgroundColor(withAlpha(CB_RANGE_COLOR, established ? CB_FILL_ESTABLISHED_ALPHA : CB_FILL_FORMING_ALPHA));
+  // On the outcome bar the range already wears its outcome: amber when the break failed, purple when it held or expired.
+  const kind = reading.decision.kind;
+  const border = kind === "failed" ? CB_WARN_COLOR : kind === "heldOutside" || kind === "expired" ? CB_RANGE_COLOR : established ? trendColor(reading.trend) : withAlpha(CB_RANGE_COLOR, CB_FORMING_BORDER_ALPHA);
+  box.setBorderColor(border);
+  box.setBorderWidth(1);
+}
+
+function drawHeld(box: BoxHandle, held: HeldRange | undefined, tsOpen: number): void {
+  // A range retired on this very bar is still shown through the active snapshot; it joins the held drawing from the next bar.
+  if (held === undefined || held.closedTs >= tsOpen) {
+    hideBox(box);
+    return;
+  }
+  box.setLeftTop(held.openedTs, held.hi);
+  box.setRightBottom(held.closedTs, held.lo);
+  box.setBackgroundColor(withAlpha(CB_RANGE_COLOR, CB_FILL_HELD_ALPHA));
+  box.setBorderColor(withAlpha(held.outcome === "failed" ? CB_WARN_COLOR : CB_RANGE_COLOR, CB_HELD_BORDER_ALPHA));
+  box.setBorderWidth(1);
+}
+
+function plotMarkers(ctx: IndicatorBarCtx<CompressionBreakoutState, CompressionBreakoutSettings>, reading: Reading): void {
+  const d = reading.decision;
+  if (d.kind === "break" && d.grade >= 1) {
+    const base = d.dir > 0 ? CB_BULL_COLOR : CB_BEAR_COLOR;
+    const alpha = (d.grade === 3 ? 1 : CB_FADED_MARKER_ALPHA) * (reading.confirmed ? 1 : CB_PROVISIONAL_ALPHA);
+    ctx.markers.plotshape({
+      shape: d.dir > 0 ? "arrowUp" : "arrowDown",
+      position: d.dir > 0 ? "belowBar" : "aboveBar",
+      color: alpha === 1 ? base : withAlpha(base, alpha),
+      size: 1,
+    });
+  } else if (d.kind === "failed" && d.grade >= 1) {
+    ctx.markers.plotshape({
+      shape: "circle",
+      position: "inBar",
+      color: reading.confirmed ? CB_WARN_COLOR : withAlpha(CB_WARN_COLOR, CB_PROVISIONAL_ALPHA),
+      size: 1,
+    });
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * The edge lines' per-bar colour. The chart paints each segment with the
+ * colour of the point it starts from, so the last point of a range (the bar
+ * whose decision retires it) is painted transparent: the segment that would
+ * otherwise join this range's edge to the next range's edge never appears.
+ */
+export function edgeLineColor(state: CompressionBreakoutState): string {
+  const reading = state.reading;
+  if (reading === null) return CB_RANGE_COLOR;
+  const kind = reading.decision.kind;
+  if (kind === "failed" || kind === "heldOutside" || kind === "expired" || kind === "abandoned") return CB_EDGE_JOIN_COLOR;
+  return CB_RANGE_COLOR;
+}
+
+const LINES: readonly LineOutputSpec<CompressionBreakoutState>[] = [
+  { key: "rangeHigh", lineWidth: 1, color: edgeLineColor, recolor: (current) => current ?? CB_RANGE_COLOR },
+  { key: "rangeLow", lineWidth: 1, color: edgeLineColor, recolor: (current) => current ?? CB_RANGE_COLOR },
+];
+
+const SCHEMA: readonly SettingsFieldSchema<CompressionBreakoutSettings>[] = [
+  { kind: "numeric", key: "rangeLength", label: "Range length (bars)", min: 10, max: 50, step: 1, affects: "recompute" },
+  { kind: "staticHint", text: "The range needs twice this many bars before it can qualify. Replays every bar." },
+  { kind: "numeric", key: "minCompressionBars", label: "Minimum bars inside", min: 2, max: 30, step: 1, affects: "recompute" },
+  { kind: "staticHint", text: "Closed bars inside the range before a close outside counts as a break. Replays every bar." },
+  { kind: "numeric", key: "confirmMultiple", label: "Activity threshold (×)", min: 1, max: 3, step: 0.1, affects: "recompute" },
+  { kind: "staticHint", text: "The break bar's range and volume versus the prior 20 bars: one passing gives a faded arrow, both a solid one. Replays every bar." },
+  { kind: "numeric", key: "widthMultiple", label: "Width limit (× bar range)", min: 1, max: 4, step: 0.5, affects: "recompute", tab: "advanced" },
+  { kind: "staticHint", text: "A range qualifies when narrower than this many times the mean bar range before it. Replays every bar.", tab: "advanced" },
+  { kind: "numeric", key: "failBars", label: "Failure window (bars)", min: 1, max: 20, step: 1, affects: "recompute", tab: "advanced" },
+  { kind: "numeric", key: "holdBars", label: "Keep retired range (bars)", min: 0, max: 200, step: 1, affects: "recompute", tab: "advanced" },
+  { kind: "staticHint", text: "Bars a failed or held range stays drawn. Replays every bar.", tab: "advanced" },
+  { kind: "numeric", key: "trendFast", label: "Trend fast MA (bars)", min: 10, max: 199, step: 1, affects: "recompute", tab: "advanced" },
+  { kind: "numeric", key: "trendSlow", label: "Trend slow MA (bars)", min: 20, max: 400, step: 1, affects: "recompute", tab: "advanced" },
+  { kind: "boolean", key: "showMarkers", label: "Show break markers", affects: "recompute", tab: "visibility" },
+  { kind: "boolean", key: "showRanges", label: "Show ranges", affects: "recompute", tab: "visibility" },
+];
+
+export const compressionBreakoutIndicator: RuntimeIndicatorDef<CompressionBreakoutState, CompressionBreakoutSettings> = {
+  kind: "runtime",
+  id: "compressionBreakout",
+  label: "Compression Breakout (20)",
+  category: "technical",
+  defaultSettings: COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
+  settingsSchema: SCHEMA,
+  lines: LINES,
+  placement: { kind: "overlay" },
+  maxReplayBars: 10_080,
+  createState: (settings) => ({
+    engine: createCompressionBreakoutEngine(settings),
+    table: draw.table(),
+    boxes: Array.from({ length: CB_BOX_POOL }, () =>
+      draw.box({ time1: 0, price1: 0, time2: 0, price2: 0, borderColor: TRANSPARENT, backgroundColor: TRANSPARENT, borderWidth: 1 }),
+    ),
+    reading: null,
+  }),
+  onBar: (ctx) => {
+    const { bar, state, settings } = ctx;
+    const reading = state.engine.onBar(bar);
+    state.reading = reading;
+    if (settings.showMarkers) plotMarkers(ctx, reading);
+    if (ctx.drawNow === false) return;
+    if (settings.showRanges) {
+      drawActive(state.boxes[0], reading, settings);
+      for (let slot = 0; slot < MAX_HELD_RANGES; slot += 1) drawHeld(state.boxes[slot + 1], reading.held[slot], reading.tsOpen);
+    } else {
+      for (const box of state.boxes) hideBox(box);
+    }
+    const rows = tableRows(reading, settings);
+    for (let row = 0; row < rows.length; row += 1) state.table.cell(row, 0, { text: rows[row].text, textColor: rows[row].color });
+  },
+  readOutput: (state) => {
+    const reading = state.reading;
+    if (reading === null) {
+      return { rangeHigh: null, rangeLow: null, activeHigh: null, activeLow: null, heldCount: 0, widthRatio: null, qualified: null, trend: null, rangeAge: null, pending: 0, breakDir: 0, breakGrade: 0, gradeBasis: 0, failed: 0, heldOutside: 0, expired: 0 };
+    }
+    const d = reading.decision;
+    const isBreak = d.kind === "break";
+    // The brief's edge keys carry the snapshot whenever a range exists, forming included; `edgeLineColor` paints a
+    // range's last point transparent so successive ranges never join into a slope that never existed.
+    return {
+      rangeHigh: reading.rangeHigh,
+      rangeLow: reading.rangeLow,
+      // The same snapshot under the names the harness tests used, and the held count: the replay policy's "quiet bar"
+      // has neither an active nor a held range.
+      activeHigh: reading.rangeHigh,
+      activeLow: reading.rangeLow,
+      heldCount: reading.held.length,
+      widthRatio: reading.widthRatio,
+      qualified: reading.qualified === null ? null : reading.qualified ? 1 : 0,
+      trend: reading.trend,
+      rangeAge: reading.rangeAge,
+      pending: reading.pending,
+      breakDir: isBreak ? d.dir : 0,
+      breakGrade: isBreak ? d.grade : 0,
+      gradeBasis: isBreak ? d.basis : 0,
+      failed: d.kind === "failed" ? 1 : 0,
+      heldOutside: d.kind === "heldOutside" ? 1 : 0,
+      expired: d.kind === "expired" ? 1 : 0,
+    };
+  },
+};
