+describe("compressionBreakoutMath -- gaps, invalid tails, corrections and supersession (amendments 4, 5 and 6)", () => {
+  it("an intraday gap resets the active range (a fresh one may open at once), its true range is high − low, and nothing is held", () => {
+    const bars = series(44);
+    bars[43] = bar(46, baseSpec(43)); // three minutes later than the bar before it
+    const { readings, committed } = run(bars);
+    expect(readings[43].gap).toBe(true);
+    expect(readings[43].rangeHigh).toBeNull();
+    expect(readings[43].trueRange).toBe(110);
+    // The window still qualifies, so a fresh range opens at the gap bar's own commit; nothing is held.
+    expect(committed.active!.openedAt).toBe(43);
+    expect(committed.active!.age).toBe(0);
+    expect(committed.held).toEqual([]);
+    expect(committed.trs.at(-1)).toBe(110);
+  });
+
+  it("daily bars never reset on a calendar gap", () => {
+    const bars = series(44, () => null, DAY_MS);
+    bars[43] = bar(46, baseSpec(43), {}, DAY_MS);
+    const { readings } = run(bars);
+    expect(readings[43].gap).toBe(false);
+    expect(readings[43].rangeHigh).toBe(60_055);
+  });
+
+  it("an invalid-price tail across a gap cannot reset the range; the gap stays pending and the next valid-price bar resets, with true range high − low", () => {
+    const bars = series(46);
+    bars[43] = bar(46, baseSpec(43), { high: 1 });
+    bars[44] = bar(47, { open: 60_050, high: 60_151, low: 60_050, close: 60_150, volume: 100 }); // an overnight jump of 100
+    bars[45] = bar(48, baseSpec(45));
+    const { readings, committed } = run(bars);
+    expect(readings[43].gap).toBe(true);
+    expect(readings[43].decision.kind).toBe("invalidPrice");
+    expect(readings[43].rangeHigh).toBe(60_055);
+    expect(readings[44].gap).toBe(true);
+    expect(readings[44].rangeHigh).toBeNull();
+    expect(readings[44].decision.kind).toBe("none");
+    expect(readings[44].trueRange).toBe(101);
+    expect(readings[45].gap).toBe(false);
+    expect(readings[45].trueRange).toBe(Math.max(110, Math.abs(60_055 - 60_150), Math.abs(59_945 - 60_150)));
+    expect(committed.pendingGapReset).toBe(false);
+    // Without the pending reset the stale range would have been broken by bar 44: no event may exist.
+    expect(readings.some((reading) => reading.decision.kind === "break")).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it("non-finite intermediates never escape: overflowing volumes make the volume test untestable, overflowing prices make the range test untestable and the window unqualifiable", () => {
+    // The frames are built directly: the fixture helper's rounding would itself overflow these values to Infinity.
+    const hugeVolumes = series(47, (index) => (index === 46 ? BREAK_UP : null)).map((frame, index) => (index >= 26 && index < 46 ? ({ ...frame, volume: 1e308 } as BarFrame) : frame));
+    expect(hugeVolumes[30].volume).toBe(1e308);
+    const v = run(hugeVolumes).readings[46];
+    expect(v.avgVolume).toBeNull();
+    expect(v.decision).toMatchObject({ kind: "break", grade: 1, basis: 1 });
+    const wild = Array.from({ length: 60 }, (_, index) => ({ ...bar(index, baseSpec(index)), open: 0, high: 1e308, low: -1e308, close: 0, volume: 100 }) as BarFrame);
+    expect(wild[0].high).toBe(1e308);
+    const { readings, committed } = run(wild);
+    for (const reading of readings) {
+      expect(reading.trueRange).toBeNull();
+      expect(reading.widthRatio).toBeNull();
+      expect(reading.qualified === null || reading.qualified === false).toBe(true);
+      expect(reading.rangeHigh).toBeNull();
+      for (const value of [reading.avgRange, reading.avgVolume, reading.widthRatio]) expect(value === null || Number.isFinite(value)).toBe(true);
+    }
+    expect(committed.trs).toEqual([]);
+    expect(committed.highs).toHaveLength(20);
+    expect(committed.active).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it("a closed re-dispatch of the tail recomputes C from the untouched P: cancelled break, invalid-to-valid volume, removed failure", () => {
+    const base = series(46);
+    const breakBar = bar(46, BREAK_UP);
+    const insideBar = bar(46, baseSpec(46));
+    const engine = createCompressionBreakoutEngine(SETTINGS);
+    for (const frame of base) engine.onBar(frame);
+    expect(engine.onBar(breakBar).decision.kind).toBe("break");
+    // P is the committed state before this tail; it must survive the tail's re-dispatch untouched.
+    const pre = JSON.stringify(engine.pre);
+    expect(engine.pre.committedIndex).toBe(46);
+    const cancelled = engine.onBar(insideBar);
+    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.pre)).toBe(pre);
+    expect(cancelled.decision.kind).toBe("none");
+    expect(engine.committed.active!.broken).toBeNull();
+    expect(engine.committed.active!.age).toBe(7);
+    expect(engine.committed.lastEvent).toBeNull();
+    const clean = run([...base, insideBar]).committed;
+    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.committed)).toBe(JSON.stringify(clean));
+
+    const flip = createCompressionBreakoutEngine(SETTINGS);
+    for (const frame of base) flip.onBar(frame);
+    expect(flip.onBar({ ...breakBar, volume: Number.NaN } as BarFrame).decision).toMatchObject({ kind: "break", grade: 1, basis: 1 });
+    expect(flip.committed.volumes).toHaveLength(DENOMINATOR_LENGTH);
+    expect(flip.onBar(breakBar).decision).toMatchObject({ kind: "break", grade: 3, basis: 3 });
+    expect(JSON.stringify(flip.committed)).toBe(JSON.stringify(run([...base, breakBar]).committed));
+
+    const failure = createCompressionBreakoutEngine(SETTINGS);
+    for (const frame of [...base, breakBar]) failure.onBar(frame);
+    const inside47 = bar(47, { open: 60_300, high: 60_305, low: 59_995, close: 60_000, volume: 100 });
+    const outside47 = bar(47, { open: 60_300, high: 60_315, low: 60_295, close: 60_310, volume: 100 });
+    expect(failure.onBar(inside47).decision.kind).toBe("failed");
+    expect(failure.committed.held).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(failure.onBar(outside47).decision.kind).toBe("pending");
+    expect(failure.committed.held).toHaveLength(0);
+    expect(failure.committed.active!.broken).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(JSON.stringify(failure.committed)).toBe(JSON.stringify(run([...base, breakBar, outside47]).committed));
+  });
+
+  it("a tail superseded without closing writes nothing, and the hole it leaves is a gap", () => {
+    const base = series(46);
+    const engine = createCompressionBreakoutEngine(SETTINGS);
+    for (const frame of base) engine.onBar(frame);
+    const forming = engine.onBar({ ...bar(46, BREAK_UP), closed: false } as BarFrame);
+    expect(forming.confirmed).toBe(false);
+    expect(forming.decision.kind).toBe("break");
+    expect(engine.committed.committedIndex).toBe(46);
+    const next = engine.onBar(bar(47, baseSpec(47)));
+    expect(engine.committed.trs).not.toContain(260);
+    expect(next.gap).toBe(true);
+    expect(engine.committed.committedIndex).toBe(47);
+  });
+
+  it("a dispatch older than the tail is ignored and a forming revision never commits", () => {
+    const base = series(46);
+    const engine = createCompressionBreakoutEngine(SETTINGS);
+    for (const frame of base) engine.onBar(frame);
+    const before = JSON.stringify(engine.committed);
+    engine.onBar(base[10]);
+    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.committed)).toBe(before);
+    engine.onBar({ ...bar(46, BREAK_UP), closed: false } as BarFrame);
+    expect(JSON.stringify(engine.committed)).toBe(before);
+  });
+
+  it("works on an equity identity exactly as on crypto: no side is ever read", () => {
+    const equity = series(47, (index) => (index === 46 ? BREAK_UP : null)).map((frame) => ({ ...frame, ...DXFEED_EQUITY, buy_volume: undefined, sell_volume: undefined }) as BarFrame);
+    const { readings } = run(equity);
+    expect(readings[46].decision).toMatchObject({ kind: "break", grade: 3, basis: 3 });
+  });
+});

+import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { dirname } from "node:path";
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
+import { MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS } from "../../../chart/candleLifecycle";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import {
+  constantWidthBars,
+  formatPooledReplayProbe,
+  formatReplayProbe,
+  formatRolloverProbe,
+  openLiveSession,
+  percentilesOf,
+  probeReplay,
+  probeReplayPooled,
+  probeRollover,
+  relativeVolumeCryptoFixture,
+  timedDef,
+} from "../../../chart/validation";
+import { COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, compressionBreakoutIndicator } from "../compressionBreakout";
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});
+
+/**
+ * Success criterion 3 and protocol check 6, measured the frozen way: three
+ * untimed warm-up replays, five timed replays of the first 10,080 crypto
+ * bars, per-bar `onBar` plus `readOutput` pooled (the per-bar snapshot copy
+ * is inside the timed region), p99 by nearest rank. The forming-bar probe
+ * warms a range on 10,080 constant-width bars, then revises the next bar 500
+ * times alternating a breaking close and an inside close, so the break
+ * marker appears and disappears on every revision. Set CB_PERF_OUT=<file>
+ * to keep the JSON.
+ */
+
+const P99_CEILING_MS = 0.05;
+const FORMING_REVISIONS = 500;
+
+describe("compressionBreakout -- performance", () => {
+  const { bars, labels } = relativeVolumeCryptoFixture();
+  const timing = bars.slice(labels.timingIndices.first, labels.timingIndices.last + 1);
+
+  it("pooled onBar plus readOutput p99 under 0.05 ms over 10,080 bars; forming revisions under 1 ms; bounded state after the rollover", () => {
+    expect(timing).toHaveLength(10_080);
+    const pooled = probeReplayPooled(compressionBreakoutIndicator, timing, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, { warmupRuns: 3, timedRuns: 5 });
+    const replay = probeReplay(compressionBreakoutIndicator, timing, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
+    const rollover = probeRollover(compressionBreakoutIndicator, timing, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, { extraBars: 1_000 });
+
+    const warm = constantWidthBars({ count: 10_081 }).bars;
+    const { def: timed, samples } = timedDef(compressionBreakoutIndicator);
+    const session = openLiveSession(timed, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, {}, warm.slice(0, 10_080));
+    const next = warm[10_080];
+    let marked = 0;
+    let formingWallMs = 0;
+    try {
+      samples.length = 0;
+      const started = performance.now();
+      for (let revision = 0; revision < FORMING_REVISIONS; revision += 1) {
+        const breaking = revision % 2 === 0;
+        const frame = { ...next, open: 60_050, high: breaking ? 60_305 : 60_055, low: 60_045, close: breaking ? 60_300 : 60_050, volume: 200, closed: revision === FORMING_REVISIONS - 1 } as BarFrame;
+        session.dispatch({ bar: frame });
+        if (session.runtime.getMarkers(timed.id)!.some((call) => call.time === next.ts_open)) marked += 1;
+      }
+      formingWallMs = performance.now() - started;
+      expect(session.capture().faulted).toBe(false);
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+    const forming = percentilesOf(samples);
+    expect(forming.count).toBe(FORMING_REVISIONS);
+    expect(marked).toBe(FORMING_REVISIONS / 2);
+
+    const lines = [
+      formatPooledReplayProbe(pooled),
+      formatReplayProbe(replay),
+      `compressionBreakout forming bar, range warmed on 10,080 bars, ${FORMING_REVISIONS} revisions crossing the edge each time (${marked} marked): onBar p50 ${forming.p50.toFixed(4)} ms p99 ${forming.p99.toFixed(4)} ms max ${forming.max.toFixed(3)} ms; wall ${formingWallMs.toFixed(0)} ms`,
+      formatRolloverProbe(rollover),
+    ];
+    console.log(lines.join("\n"));
+    if (process.env.CB_PERF_OUT) {
+      mkdirSync(dirname(process.env.CB_PERF_OUT), { recursive: true });
+      writeFileSync(process.env.CB_PERF_OUT, `${JSON.stringify({ node: process.version, pooled, replay, rollover, forming: { revisions: FORMING_REVISIONS, marked, onBar: forming, wallMs: formingWallMs } }, null, 2)}\n`);
+    }
+    expect(pooled.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(pooled.pooled.count).toBe(10_080 * 5);
+    expect(pooled.pooled.p99).toBeLessThan(P99_CEILING_MS);
+    expect(replay.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(replay.onBar.p99).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5);
+    expect(replay.wallMs).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2_000);
+    expect(forming.p99).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
+    expect(rollover.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(rollover.retainedBars).toBe(MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS);
+    expect(rollover.outputLengths).toEqual({ rangeHigh: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS, rangeLow: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS });
+    expect(rollover.markersBeforeRetention).toBe(0);
+  }, 600_000);
+});

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import { compressionBreakoutBars, replayCapture } from "../../../chart/validation";
+import { COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, compressionBreakoutIndicator } from "../compressionBreakout";
+import { createCompressionBreakoutEngine } from "../compressionBreakoutMath";
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});
+
+/**
+ * Brief amendments 2 and 3, the pinned seed-7 timeline: the range opens on the
+ * qualifying committed bar with age 0 (index 39 at the earliest), is eligible
+ * to break once six later committed bars closed inside (index 46 at the
+ * earliest), breaks at 220 with both tests passed, is pending 221..225, is held
+ * outside at 225 with its edges still reported on that bar, and reports no
+ * range from 226 until a new one qualifies.
+ */
+describe("compressionBreakout -- the pinned seed-7 timeline", () => {
+  const { bars, labels } = compressionBreakoutBars({ seed: 7 });
+  const capture = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutIndicator, bars, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
+  const out = capture.outputs;
+
+  it("does not fault and emits every output key", () => {
+    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+    for (const key of ["rangeHigh", "rangeLow"]) expect(out[key]).toHaveLength(bars.length);
+  });
+
+  it("breaks at the labelled bar with grade 3 and basis 3, pulses only there, pends 221..225 and is held outside at 225", () => {
+    const b = labels.breakoutIndex;
+    expect(b).toBe(220);
+    const engine = createCompressionBreakoutEngine(COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
+    const readings = bars.map((bar) => engine.onBar(bar));
+    expect(readings[b].decision.kind).toBe("break");
+    expect(readings[b].decision.dir).toBe(1);
+    expect(readings[b].decision.grade).toBe(3);
+    expect(readings[b].decision.basis).toBe(3);
+    expect(readings[b].pending).toBe(0);
+    expect(readings[b].rangeHigh).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(readings[b].rangeHigh!).toBeLessThanOrEqual(labels.compressionHigh);
+    expect(readings[b].rangeLow!).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(labels.compressionLow);
+    expect(bars[b].close).toBeGreaterThan(readings[b].rangeHigh!);
+    for (let index = b + 1; index <= b + 5; index += 1) {
+      expect(readings[index].pending).toBe(1);
+      expect(readings[index].pendingBars).toBe(index - b);
+      expect(readings[index].rangeHigh).toBe(readings[b].rangeHigh);
+    }
+    for (let index = b + 1; index <= b + 4; index += 1) expect(readings[index].decision.kind).toBe("pending");
+    expect(readings[b + 5].decision.kind).toBe("heldOutside");
+    expect(readings[b + 6].pending).toBe(0);
+    expect(readings[b + 6].rangeHigh).toBeNull();
+    expect(readings.filter((reading) => reading.decision.kind === "break")).toHaveLength(readings.filter((reading) => reading.decision.kind === "break" && reading.tsOpen === bars[b].ts_open).length);
+    // lastEvent updates at the event bar's commit.
+    expect(readings[b].lastEvent?.kind).toBe("break");
+    expect(readings[b + 5].lastEvent?.kind).toBe("heldOutside");
+    // The held range is drawn from 226 and freed at 266's commit (holdBars 40).
+    expect(readings[b + 6].held.some((held) => held.closedTs === bars[b + 5].ts_open)).toBe(true);
+    expect(readings[b + 45].held.some((held) => held.closedTs === bars[b + 5].ts_open)).toBe(true);
+    expect(readings[b + 46].held.some((held) => held.closedTs === bars[b + 5].ts_open)).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it("opens no range before index 39 and never before 2 × rangeLength valid committed bars; the earliest eligible break is 46", () => {
+    const engine = createCompressionBreakoutEngine(COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
+    const readings = bars.map((bar) => engine.onBar(bar));
+    for (let index = 0; index < 39; index += 1) {
+      expect(readings[index].rangeReady).toBe(false);
+      expect(readings[index].rangeHigh).toBeNull();
+      expect(readings[index].qualified).toBeNull();
+    }
+    expect(readings[39].rangeReady).toBe(true);
+    const firstOpen = readings.findIndex((reading) => reading.rangeHigh !== null);
+    expect(firstOpen).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(40); // reported one bar after the opening commit
+    const firstBreak = readings.findIndex((reading) => reading.decision.kind === "break");
+    expect(firstBreak === -1 || firstBreak >= 46).toBe(true);
+    // age 0 at open: the first reading that shows the range reports age 0.
+    expect(readings[firstOpen].rangeAge).toBe(0);
+  });
+});

+import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";

+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();

+const SETTINGS: CompressionBreakoutSettings = COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS;
+const SEED7 = compressionBreakoutBars({ seed: 7 });
+const FAILED9 = compressionFailedBreakBars({ seed: 9 });
+const HELD9 = compressionContinuationBars({ seed: 9 });
+
+function noNaN(outputs: Outputs): void {
+  for (const values of Object.values(outputs)) {
+    for (const value of values) expect(value === null || Number.isFinite(value)).toBe(true);
+  }
+}
+
+/** Amendment 1: the first retained bar with no active and no held range once the denominators and the trend window lie inside retention. */
+function quietBarIndex(outputs: Outputs, from: number, settings: CompressionBreakoutSettings): number {
+  const warm = from + Math.max(2 * settings.rangeLength, 20, settings.trendSlow + 5);
+  for (let index = warm; index < outputs.activeHigh.length; index += 1) {
+    if (outputs.activeHigh[index] === null && outputs.heldCount[index] === 0) return index;
+  }
+  return -1;
+}

+describe("compressionBreakout -- no future data: truncation invariance (protocol check 1)", () => {
+  it("passes at every cut of the breakout, failed-break, continuation and trend fixtures, seams and markers alike", () => {
+    for (const { bars } of [SEED7, FAILED9, HELD9, trendBars({ count: 600 })]) {
+      const seams = checkTruncationInvariance(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars, SETTINGS);
+      expect(seams.checked).toBe(bars.length);
+      expect(seams.ok).toBe(true);
+      const product = checkTruncationInvariance(compressionBreakoutIndicator, bars, SETTINGS);
+      console.log(summarizeTruncation(product));
+      expect(product.markerViolations).toEqual([]);
+      expect(product.ok).toBe(true);
+    }
+  }, 300_000);
+});
+
+describe("compressionBreakout -- replay versus live equivalence (protocol check 2)", () => {
+  it("ends on the same committed seams and markers whether fed live with five forming revisions per bar or replayed", () => {
+    for (const { bars } of [SEED7, FAILED9, { bars: constantWidthBars({ count: 700 }).bars }]) {
+      const seams = checkLiveReplayEquivalence(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars, SETTINGS, { revisionsPerBar: 5 });
+      expect(seams.mutations.filter((mutation) => mutation === "REPLACED_EDGE")).toHaveLength(bars.length * 5);
+      expect(seams.equal).toBe(true);
+      noNaN(seams.live.outputs);
+      const product = checkLiveReplayEquivalence(compressionBreakoutIndicator, bars, SETTINGS, { revisionsPerBar: 5 });
+      console.log(summarizeEquivalence("compressionBreakout", product));
+      expect(product.equal).toBe(true);
+    }
+    expect(replayCapture(compressionBreakoutIndicator, SEED7.bars, SETTINGS).markers.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+  }, 300_000);
+
+  it("a replay whose tail is a forming bar keeps the tail provisional: its marker is faded and nothing is committed for it", () => {
+    const bars = SEED7.bars.slice(0, 221);
+    const tail = { ...bars[220], closed: false } as BarFrame;
+    const capture = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutIndicator, [...bars.slice(0, 220), tail], SETTINGS);
+    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+    const marker = capture.markers.find((call) => call.time === tail.ts_open);
+    expect(marker).toBeDefined();
+    expect(marker!.color.startsWith("rgba(")).toBe(true);
+    const engine = createCompressionBreakoutEngine(SETTINGS);
+    for (const bar of [...bars.slice(0, 220), tail]) engine.onBar(bar);
+    expect(engine.committed.committedIndex).toBe(220);
+    expect(engine.current!.confirmed).toBe(false);
+    expect(engine.current!.decision.kind).toBe("break");
+  });
+});

+describe("compressionBreakout -- gap, duplicate, late and corrected bars (protocol check 3, amendments 4 to 6)", () => {
+  it("duplicate closed bar is idempotent, inside the failure window included, and adds no marker", () => {
+    for (const index of [221, 100]) {
+      const report = checkDuplicateClosedIdempotent(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, SEED7.bars, SETTINGS, index);
+      expect(report.duplicateMutation).toBe("REPLACED_EDGE");
+      expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+    }
+    const budget = countMarkersOver(compressionBreakoutIndicator, duplicateClosedArrivals(FAILED9.bars, 220).arrivals, SETTINGS);
+    expect(budget.total).toBe(countMarkers(compressionBreakoutIndicator, FAILED9.bars, SETTINGS).total);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("late bar rebuilds to the replay", () => {
+    const report = checkRebuiltEquivalence(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, SEED7.bars, SETTINGS, 150);
+    expect(report.sawRebuilt).toBe(true);
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+    expect(report.live.outputs.activeHigh).toHaveLength(SEED7.bars.length);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("a correction to an older bar arrives as a REBUILT and every successor equals a fresh replay", () => {
+    const arrivals: Arrival[] = SEED7.bars.map((bar) => ({ bar }));
+    const corrected = { ...SEED7.bars[150], volume: SEED7.bars[150].volume * 4 } as BarFrame;
+    arrivals.push({ bar: corrected, label: "correction" });
+    const report = checkArrivalsAgainstReplay(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, arrivals, SETTINGS);
+    expect(report.mutations.at(-1)).toBe("REBUILT");
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("the tail corrections of the brief: cancelled break, volume invalid then valid, failure removed; each equals a clean feed of the corrected series", () => {
+    const b = SEED7.labels.breakoutIndex;
+    const prefix: Arrival[] = SEED7.bars.slice(0, b).map((bar) => ({ bar }));
+    const rest = (from: number): Arrival[] => SEED7.bars.slice(from).map((bar) => ({ bar }));
+    // The range's frozen high (it opened inside the contraction) is below the labelled compression high: "inside" means inside the frozen edges.
+    const edge = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, SEED7.bars, SETTINGS).outputs.activeHigh[b] as number;
+    expect(edge).toBeLessThanOrEqual(SEED7.labels.compressionHigh);
+    // 1. The break bar closes again inside the range: the event disappears and the range stays active.
+    const inside220 = { ...SEED7.bars[b], close: edge - 1, low: Math.min(SEED7.bars[b].low, edge - 1) } as BarFrame;
+    const cancelled = checkArrivalsAgainstReplay(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, [...prefix, { bar: SEED7.bars[b] }, { bar: inside220, label: "closed again" }, ...rest(b + 1)], SETTINGS);
+    expect(cancelled.mutations[b + 1]).toBe("REPLACED_EDGE");
+    expect(cancelled.equal).toBe(true);
+    expect(cancelled.live.outputs.breakDir[b]).toBe(0);
+    expect(cancelled.live.outputs.activeHigh[b + 1]).not.toBeNull();
+    const cleanInside = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, SEED7.bars.map((bar, index) => (index === b ? inside220 : bar)), SETTINGS);
+    expect(diffOutputs(cancelled.live.outputs, cleanInside.outputs)).toEqual([]);
+    // 2. The break bar first arrives with an invalid volume (range only, vol n/a), then valid (range+vol).
+    const noVolume = { ...SEED7.bars[b], volume: Number.NaN } as BarFrame;
+    const flipped = checkArrivalsAgainstReplay(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, [...prefix, { bar: noVolume }, { bar: SEED7.bars[b], label: "valid again" }, ...rest(b + 1)], SETTINGS);
+    expect(flipped.equal).toBe(true);
+    expect(flipped.live.outputs.breakGrade[b]).toBe(3);
+    const onlyInvalid = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, SEED7.bars.map((bar, index) => (index === b ? noVolume : bar)), SETTINGS);
+    expect(onlyInvalid.outputs.breakGrade[b]).toBe(1);
+    expect(onlyInvalid.outputs.gradeBasis[b]).toBe(1);
+    // 3. The bar after the break first closes inside (failure), then outside (pending again).
+    const inside221 = { ...SEED7.bars[b + 1], close: edge - 1, low: Math.min(SEED7.bars[b + 1].low, edge - 2) } as BarFrame;
+    const removed = checkArrivalsAgainstReplay(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, [...prefix, { bar: SEED7.bars[b] }, { bar: inside221 }, { bar: SEED7.bars[b + 1], label: "outside again" }, ...rest(b + 2)], SETTINGS);
+    expect(removed.equal).toBe(true);
+    expect(removed.live.outputs.failed[b + 1]).toBe(0);
+    expect(removed.live.outputs.pending[b + 1]).toBe(1);
+    expect(removed.live.outputs.heldOutside[b + 5]).toBe(1);
+    const onlyInside = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, SEED7.bars.map((bar, index) => (index === b + 1 ? inside221 : bar)), SETTINGS);
+    expect(onlyInside.outputs.failed[b + 1]).toBe(1);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("time gap below one day: the first bar after the hole reads a reset range, its true range is high − low, nothing earlier changes", () => {
+    const { bars, labels } = timeGapBars({ count: 600 });
+    const gapped = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars, SETTINGS);
+    const prefix = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars.slice(0, labels.gapAfterIndex + 1), SETTINGS);
+    expect(diffOutputs(gapped.outputs, prefix.outputs, 0, labels.gapAfterIndex)).toEqual([]);
+    expect(gapped.outputs.activeHigh[labels.firstBarAfterGapIndex]).toBeNull();
+    const engine = createCompressionBreakoutEngine(SETTINGS);
+    let after = null as ReturnType<typeof engine.onBar> | null;
+    bars.forEach((bar, index) => {
+      const reading = engine.onBar(bar);
+      if (index === labels.firstBarAfterGapIndex) after = reading;
+    });
+    const gapBar = bars[labels.firstBarAfterGapIndex];
+    expect(after!.gap).toBe(true);
+    expect(after!.trueRange).toBe(gapBar.high - gapBar.low);
+    noNaN(gapped.outputs);
+  });
+
+  it("the gap rule applies only below one-day intervals: a three-day hole in daily bars keeps the range, a three-minute hole in 1m bars resets it", () => {
+    for (const [intervalMs, expectReset] of [
+      [60_000, true],
+      [DAY_MS, false],
+    ] as const) {
+      const { bars } = constantWidthBars({ count: 120, intervalMs });
+      const gapped = bars.map((bar, index) => (index >= 80 ? ({ ...bar, ts_open: bar.ts_open + 2 * intervalMs } as BarFrame) : bar));
+      const capture = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, gapped, SETTINGS);
+      expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+      expect(capture.outputs.activeHigh[79]).not.toBeNull();
+      if (expectReset) expect(capture.outputs.activeHigh[80]).toBeNull();
+      else expect(capture.outputs.activeHigh[80]).toBe(capture.outputs.activeHigh[79]);
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("equity bars at one-minute and daily intervals run without a fault, read no side, and reset only on the intraday overnight hole", () => {
+    for (const intervalMs of [60_000, DAY_MS]) {
+      const { bars } = equityUnsidedBars({ intervalMs, count: 400 });
+      const capture = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars, SETTINGS);
+      expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+      noNaN(capture.outputs);
+      expect(capture.outputs.qualified.slice(39).some((value) => value !== null)).toBe(true);
+    }
+    const source = readFileSync(join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..", "compressionBreakoutMath.ts"), "utf8") + readFileSync(join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..", "compressionBreakout.ts"), "utf8");
+    expect(source.includes("buy_volume")).toBe(false);
+    expect(source.includes("barSides")).toBe(false);
+    expect(source.includes("provider ===")).toBe(false);
+  });
+});

+describe("compressionBreakout -- warm-up, empty input and NaN (protocol check 4)", () => {
+  it("readiness per component: qualification from index 39, edges only once established, trend from index 205, never NaN or a stand-in zero", () => {
+    const capture = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, SEED7.bars, SETTINGS);

+describe("compressionBreakout -- marker budget (protocol check 5)", () => {
+  it("stays under the signal ceiling with at most one marker per bar on the range and trend controls, and reports the counts", () => {
+    const lines: string[] = [];
+    for (const regime of ["range", "trend"] as const) {
+      let total = 0;
+      for (let seed = 7; seed <= 11; seed += 1) {
+        const { bars } = regime === "range" ? rangeBars({ seed, count: 1_000 }) : trendBars({ seed, count: 1_000 });
+        const budget = countMarkers(compressionBreakoutIndicator, bars, SETTINGS);
+        const verdict = judgeBudget(budget, 40, 1);
+        expect(verdict.ok).toBe(true);
+        total += budget.total;
+        lines.push(`${regime} seed ${seed}: ${budget.total} markers (${budget.per1000Bars.toFixed(1)} per 1,000), max per bar ${budget.maxPerBar}`);
+      }
+      lines.push(`${regime} control, 5 × 1,000 bars: ${(total / 5).toFixed(1)} markers per 1,000`);
+    }
+    console.log(lines.join("\n"));
+  }, 120_000);
+});
+
+describe("compressionBreakout -- Historical Mode policy is honest (protocol check 10)", () => {
+  it("declares OHLCV_POLICY, is available wherever candles are, and a candle-only replay produces real values", () => {
+    expect(HISTORICAL_INDICATOR_POLICIES.compressionBreakout).toEqual({ requiredCapabilities: ["ohlcv"] });
+    expect(historicalIndicatorAvailability("compressionBreakout", new Set(["ohlcv"]))).toEqual({ available: true, reason: null });
+    expect(historicalIndicatorAvailability("compressionBreakout", new Set([])).available).toBe(false);
+    const capture = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, SEED7.bars, SETTINGS);
+    expect(capture.outputs.breakDir[220]).toBe(1);
+    expect(capture.markers.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("compressionBreakout -- replay policy (amendment 1)", () => {
+  function compareFromQuiet(bars: readonly BarFrame[], offset: number, label: string): number {
+    const live = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars, SETTINGS);
+    const rebuilt = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars.slice(offset), SETTINGS);
+    const quiet = quietBarIndex(live.outputs, offset, SETTINGS);
+    expect(quiet).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
+    let compared = 0;
+    for (let index = quiet; index < bars.length; index += 1) {
+      for (const key of SEAM_KEYS) expect(rebuilt.outputs[key][index - offset]).toBe(live.outputs[key][index]);
+      compared += 1;
+    }
+    const liveMarkers = live.markers.filter((call) => call.time >= bars[quiet].ts_open).map((call) => JSON.stringify(call));
+    const rebuiltMarkers = rebuilt.markers.filter((call) => call.time >= bars[quiet].ts_open).map((call) => JSON.stringify(call));
+    expect(rebuiltMarkers).toEqual(liveMarkers);
+    console.log(`${label}: quiet bar ${quiet}, ${compared} bars compared equal after it`);
+    return compared;
+  }
+
+  it("outputs, events and markers are equal from the first quiet bar on the breakout and control fixtures, with a non-zero compared count", () => {
+    expect(compareFromQuiet(compressionBreakoutBars({ seed: 7, count: 1_000 }).bars, 60, "breakout seed 7, offset 60")).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(compareFromQuiet(trendBars({ seed: 7, count: 1_200 }).bars, 100, "trend control, offset 100")).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(compareFromQuiet(rangeBars({ seed: 8, count: 1_500 }).bars, 137, "range control, offset 137")).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("before any quiet bar the difference is the range phase, pinned on the constant-width fixture at offsets 0, 137 and 500", () => {
+    const { bars, labels } = constantWidthBars();
+    const live = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars, SETTINGS);
+    const opensOf = (outputs: Outputs): number[] => outputs.rangeAge.map((age, index) => (age === 0 ? index - 1 : -1)).filter((index) => index >= 0);
+    const liveOpens = opensOf(live.outputs);
+    expect(liveOpens).toEqual([39, 39 + RANGE_LIFETIME + 1, 39 + 2 * (RANGE_LIFETIME + 1), 39 + 3 * (RANGE_LIFETIME + 1), 39 + 4 * (RANGE_LIFETIME + 1), 39 + 5 * (RANGE_LIFETIME + 1)].filter((index) => index < bars.length));
+    expect(quietBarIndex(live.outputs, 0, SETTINGS)).toBe(-1);
+    for (const offset of labels.rebuildOffsets) {
+      const rebuilt = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars.slice(offset), SETTINGS);
+      const rebuiltOpens = opensOf(rebuilt.outputs).map((index) => index + offset);
+      expect(rebuiltOpens).toEqual(liveOpens.map((index) => index + offset).filter((index) => index < bars.length));
+      let ageDiffers = 0;
+      for (let index = offset + 40; index < bars.length; index += 1) {
+        // The edges are the band's constants on both sides; only the phase (age, expiry) differs when the offset is not a multiple of the period.
+        if (rebuilt.outputs.activeHigh[index - offset] !== null && live.outputs.activeHigh[index] !== null) {
+          expect(rebuilt.outputs.activeHigh[index - offset]).toBe(live.outputs.activeHigh[index]);
+          expect(rebuilt.outputs.activeLow[index - offset]).toBe(live.outputs.activeLow[index]);
+        }
+        if (rebuilt.outputs.rangeAge[index - offset] !== live.outputs.rangeAge[index]) ageDiffers += 1;
+      }
+      if (offset === 0) expect(ageDiffers).toBe(0);
+      else expect(ageDiffers).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+      console.log(`constant width, offset ${offset}: rebuilt opens at ${rebuiltOpens.join(", ")}; live opens at ${liveOpens.join(", ")}; age differs on ${ageDiffers} bars`);
+    }
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("a coil older than retention, rebuilt from index 300: both instances break at 740 with the same grade, edges may differ before, and they agree after the hold", () => {
+    const { bars, labels } = longCompressionBars();
+    const live = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars, SETTINGS);
+    const rebuilt = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars.slice(labels.rebuildFromIndex), SETTINGS);
+    const b = labels.breakoutIndex;
+    const at = (outputs: Outputs, key: string, index: number, offset = 0): number | null => outputs[key][index - offset];
+    expect(at(live.outputs, "breakDir", b)).toBe(1);
+    expect(at(rebuilt.outputs, "breakDir", b, labels.rebuildFromIndex)).toBe(1);
+    expect(at(rebuilt.outputs, "breakGrade", b, labels.rebuildFromIndex)).toBe(at(live.outputs, "breakGrade", b));
+    const edgeDiffers = at(live.outputs, "activeHigh", b) !== at(rebuilt.outputs, "activeHigh", b, labels.rebuildFromIndex);
+    console.log(`long coil: live edges ${at(live.outputs, "activeLow", b)}..${at(live.outputs, "activeHigh", b)}, rebuilt edges ${at(rebuilt.outputs, "activeLow", b, labels.rebuildFromIndex)}..${at(rebuilt.outputs, "activeHigh", b, labels.rebuildFromIndex)} (${edgeDiffers ? "differ" : "equal"})`);
+    expect(compareFromQuiet(bars, labels.rebuildFromIndex, "long coil, offset 300")).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+  }, 120_000);
+});

+import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
+import type { CompressionBreakoutLabels, Outputs } from "../../../chart/validation";
+
+/**
+ * The scoring of the brief and amendment 8, identical for every def:
+ * matched = a break event inside `[breakoutIndex, breakoutIndex + 2]`; hit =
+ * the first matched event carries the labelled direction; wrong-direction = a
+ * break event opposing the label inside that window; early = before the
+ * window (split at `compressionStart`); unmatched = after it; missed = no
+ * matched event; level valid = on a hit, the frozen edges lie inside the
+ * labelled compression range and the close is beyond the tested edge.
+ * Filtering keeps only events whose bar passes the identical activity test
+ * (true range or volume at or above 1.5 times the mean of the preceding 20
+ * bars), computed here from the bars alone, independently of any def.
+ */
+
+export interface BreakEvent {
+  index: number;
+  dir: number;
+  grade: number | null;
+}
+
+export function breakEventsOf(outputs: Outputs): BreakEvent[] {
+  const events: BreakEvent[] = [];
+  const dirs = outputs.breakDir ?? [];
+  const grades = outputs.breakGrade;
+  dirs.forEach((dir, index) => {
+    if (dir !== null && dir !== 0) events.push({ index, dir, grade: grades === undefined ? null : (grades[index] ?? null) });
+  });
+  return events;
+}
+
+/** The identical activity filter: true range or volume at least 1.5× the mean of the preceding 20 bars. */
+export function activityFlags(bars: readonly BarFrame[], multiple = 1.5): boolean[] {
+  const flags: boolean[] = [];
+  const trs: number[] = [];
+  const vols: number[] = [];
+  let prevClose: number | null = null;
+  for (const bar of bars) {
+    const tr = prevClose === null ? bar.high - bar.low : Math.max(bar.high - bar.low, Math.abs(bar.high - prevClose), Math.abs(bar.low - prevClose));
+    const mean = (values: number[]): number | null => (values.length < 20 ? null : values.slice(-20).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / 20);
+    const avgTr = mean(trs);
+    const avgVol = mean(vols);
+    const rangePass = avgTr !== null && avgTr > 0 && tr >= multiple * avgTr;
+    const volumePass = avgVol !== null && avgVol > 0 && bar.volume >= multiple * avgVol;
+    flags.push(rangePass || volumePass);
+    trs.push(tr);
+    vols.push(bar.volume);
+    prevClose = bar.close;
+  }
+  return flags;
+}
+
+export interface Score {
+  hit: 0 | 1;
+  wrongDirection: number;
+  matched: number;
+  early: number;
+  earlyLead: number;
+  earlyCompression: number;
+  unmatched: number;
+  missed: 0 | 1;
+  /** 1 when the hit's edges and close satisfy the level rule; 0 when a hit fails it; null without a hit or without edges. */
+  levelValid: 0 | 1 | null;
+}
+
+export function scoreBreakout(
+  bars: readonly BarFrame[],
+  outputs: Outputs,
+  labels: CompressionBreakoutLabels,
+  filter: readonly boolean[] | null = null,
+  tolerance = 2,
+): Score {
+  const events = breakEventsOf(outputs).filter((event) => filter === null || filter[event.index]);
+  const b = labels.breakoutIndex;
+  const score: Score = { hit: 0, wrongDirection: 0, matched: 0, early: 0, earlyLead: 0, earlyCompression: 0, unmatched: 0, missed: 0, levelValid: null };
+  let first: BreakEvent | null = null;
+  for (const event of events) {
+    if (event.index >= b && event.index <= b + tolerance) {
+      score.matched += 1;
+      if (event.dir !== labels.direction) score.wrongDirection += 1;
+      if (first === null) first = event;
+    } else if (event.index < b) {
+      score.early += 1;
+      if (event.index < labels.compressionStart) score.earlyLead += 1;
+      else score.earlyCompression += 1;
+    } else {
+      score.unmatched += 1;
+    }
+  }
+  if (first === null) {
+    score.missed = 1;
+    return score;
+  }
+  if (first.dir === labels.direction) {
+    score.hit = 1;
+    const hi = outputs.activeHigh?.[first.index];
+    const lo = outputs.activeLow?.[first.index];
+    if (hi !== undefined && lo !== undefined && hi !== null && lo !== null) {
+      const close = bars[first.index].close;
+      const beyond = labels.direction > 0 ? close > hi : close < lo;
+      score.levelValid = hi <= labels.compressionHigh + 1e-9 && lo >= labels.compressionLow - 1e-9 && beyond ? 1 : 0;
+    }
+  }
+  return score;
+}
+
+export function addScores(total: Record<string, number>, score: Score): void {
+  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(score)) {
+    if (value === null) continue;
+    total[key] = (total[key] ?? 0) + value;
+  }
+  if (score.levelValid !== null) total.levelChecked = (total.levelChecked ?? 0) + 1;
+}
+
+/** The labelled outcome: a break at `breakoutIndex` and the pulse at `outcomeIndex`. */
+export function outcomeHit(outputs: Outputs, breakoutIndex: number, outcomeIndex: number, key: "failed" | "heldOutside"): boolean {
+  const broke = (outputs.breakDir?.[breakoutIndex] ?? 0) !== 0;
+  const outcome = (outputs[key]?.[outcomeIndex] ?? 0) === 1;
+  return broke && outcome;
+}

+
+/**
+ * The runtime records a per-bar value only for the keys a def declares as
+ * `lines`, so the harness's truncation and equivalence checks would otherwise

+ * `heldCount`, the trend and the width ratio.
+ */

+};

+# Compression Breakout (20)
+
+[Handbook](../../README.md) · [Indicator index](README.md) ·
+[Data for indicator authors](data-for-indicator-authors.md) ·
+[Setting presets](setting-presets.md)
+
+Code: `apps/web/src/lib/indicators/compression-breakout/`
+(`compressionBreakout.ts` is the registry definition, `compressionBreakoutMath.ts`
+the pure state machine, `tests/` the evidence, including the test-only
+comparator defs). Registry id `compressionBreakout`, label "Compression
+Breakout (20)", category technical, overlay. The code wins over this page.
+
+## The question it answers
+
+"Did price leave an established compression range with activity behind the
+break bar, and did it come back inside?"
+
+A compression range is a committed 20-bar high/low range narrower than twice
+the mean true range of the 20 bars before it: price has stayed inside a band
+narrower than two of its recent bars. Its edges freeze the moment it opens.
+Once six later closed bars stayed inside, a close outside is a break, graded by
+what the break bar did (its true range and its volume against the preceding 20
+bars). A close back inside within five bars is a failed break; otherwise the
+break is "held outside 5 bars". Trend (MA 50 against MA 200) colours a border
+and one table row and never decides anything. Descriptive only: "range+vol"
+says both activity tests passed on the break bar, "held outside" says the
+failure window expired. Neither says anything about profit, and a return
+inside is a failure of this breakout rule, not evidence for fading it.
+
+## Calculation
+
+One bar at a time; `onBar` never rebuilds from an array. Three named states:
+`P`, the committed state before the current bar, kept untouched while that bar
+is the tail; `W`, the working view every evaluation reads (`P` plus the gap
+rule, never written); `C`, the committed state after the bar's first closed
+dispatch, which seeds the next `ts_open`.
+
+1. **Validity.** A bar's price is valid when open, high, low and close are
+   finite with `low ≤ open, close ≤ high`; its volume when finite and at least
+   zero. An invalid-price bar contributes nothing except one tick of the clock
+   (lifetime, failure window, hold countdown): no ring entry, no range change,
+   no age, no event, status `No usable price`. An invalid volume keeps the
+   price context; the bar enters no volume observation and its volume cannot
+   be tested (`vol n/a`). Missing bars contribute nothing. Means span valid
+   observations across gaps.
+2. **Rings of committed valid bars.** Highs and lows (20), true ranges (40),
+   volumes (20), closes (`trendSlow`), the fast MA's last six values. No
+   library stream drives an event: a never-closed bar left in one would change
+   range creation. True range uses the previous committed close; on the first
+   bar and on the first bar after a gap it is `high − low`, so an overnight
+   jump never enters a reference or a denominator.
+3. **Gap.** Below one-day bars, a `ts_open` more than one interval after the
+   last committed bar drops the active range on `W` (status `Gap · range
+   reset`); held ranges stay. Daily and weekly bars never reset on calendar
+   gaps. An invalid-price bar skips the reset with every other commit step.
+4. **Qualification.** The window of the last 20 committed highs and lows
+   qualifies when `width ≤ widthMultiple × mean true range of the 20 valid bars
+   before the window`. Readiness is 40 valid committed bars (zero-based index
+   39 at the earliest); `qualified` and the width ratio are `null` before.
+5. **Opening and establishment.** A range opens at the commit of a qualifying
+   bar with no active range and no event, with `age` 0 and its edges frozen
+   at the window's extremes. Each later committed bar whose close stays inside
+   adds one to `age`. A close outside before `age ≥ minCompressionBars` abandons
+   the range silently (nothing held, no event). The earliest eligible break at
+   defaults is index 46.
+6. **Break and grade.** Eligible and `close > hi` (or `< lo`): a break. The
+   denominators are the means of the preceding 20 valid true ranges and
+   volumes, available only when 20 exist and the mean is positive.
+   `gradeBasis` says which components could be tested on this bar
+   (denominator available and the bar's value valid): 0 none, 1 range, 2
+   volume, 3 both. `breakGrade`: 3 both passed (`range+vol`), 1 range only,
+   2 volume only, 0 none. The threshold is `confirmMultiple` (1.5) on both.
+7. **Outcome.** From the bar after the break: a close back inside the frozen
+   edges, or beyond the opposite edge, is a failure; otherwise, `failBars`
+   committed bars after the break (the last included, invalid bars counted)
+   the break is held outside. Either retires the range into the held list for
+   `holdBars` committed bars (`holdBars` 0 keeps nothing). No widening, no
+   re-arm: a new range must qualify and establish again. A range that never
+   breaks expires 500 committed bars after it opened and is held like a
+   held-outside one.
+8. **Order of a commit.** Held countdown first (a range at zero is freed, the
+   rest count down); then the rings; then the decision (break, failure, hold,
+   expiry, abandonment, pending or inside); then a new range may open; then
+   the clock advances.
+9. **Snapshot and corrections.** `readOutput` reads a snapshot taken after the
+   decision and before any retirement, so the edges are still reported on the
+   outcome bar and `pending` is 1 from the bar after the break through the
+   outcome bar. A forming revision evaluates against `P` only. A closed
+   re-dispatch of the tail recomputes `C` from `P`: a cancelled break, a
+   removed failure, a changed grade and a validity flip in either field are one
+   rule. A correction to an older bar reaches the indicator as a full replay.
+   A tail superseded without closing writes nothing; the hole it leaves is a
+   gap.
+10. **Trend.** `up` if the close is above the slow MA and the fast MA rose over
+    the last five committed bars; `down` on the mirror; `flat` otherwise;
+    unknown until `trendSlow + 5` valid closes, and off when `trendFast ≥
+    trendSlow`.
+
+### Outputs
+
+Line outputs `rangeHigh` and `rangeLow`: the snapshot edges whenever a range
+exists, forming included; a range's last point (its outcome or abandonment
+bar) is painted transparent so the line never joins one range to the next
+with a slope that never existed. Numeric seams for the harness and for later
+layers: `activeHigh`, `activeLow` (the same snapshot under the names the
+harness tests use), `heldCount`, `widthRatio`, `qualified`, `trend`, `rangeAge`, `pending`, and
+the event pulses `breakDir`, `breakGrade`, `gradeBasis`, `failed`,
+`heldOutside`, `expired`, non-zero on the event bar only. The L1 tape tally
+grades intensity on the `breakDir` bar; the L2 Break Defense Monitor watches
+displayed liquidity at the edges while the range is established.
+
+## What the trader sees
+
+A neutral purple box over the compression range: faint with a faded border
+while forming, fuller with a 1 px border in the trend colour once established
+(bull/bear pair, purple when flat or unknown); direction is carried only by
+the arrows. A retired range stays drawn for `holdBars` bars with a fainter
+fill, purple border when held outside or expired, amber when failed. The two
+edge lines while a range exists. Markers: `arrowUp` below the break bar or
+`arrowDown` above it, solid when both activity tests passed, faded when one
+did, none when neither; an amber circle in the bar on a marked break's
+failure. One decision per bar, so at most one marker per bar. A four-row table
+top right: `Range 66878.17–67237.22` (low–high at the instrument's precision,
+six significant digits if the row would exceed 30 characters) or `No range`;
+`Trend ↑ · MA 50/200` / `Trend ↓` / `Trend flat` / `Trend warming · 120 of
+205` / `Trend off · fast ≥ slow`; the state (`No usable price`, `Gap · range
+reset`, `Failed break`, `Held outside · 5 bars`, `Range expired · 500 bars`,
+`Break ↑ · range+vol` with the bases `range only`, `vol only`, `unconfirmed`,
+`range, vol n/a`, `vol n/a`, `vol, range n/a`, `range n/a`, `no baseline`,
+`Holding · 2 of 5 bars`, `Range · 9 bars · width 1.6×`, `Range forming · 3
+of 6`, `Watching · width 2.6×`); and the last event (`Last ↑ 12:34 ·
+range+vol`, `Last failed ↑ 12:34`, `Last held ↑ 12:34 · 5 bars`, `Last expired
+12:34`, `—`). During warm-up the table shows the single row `Warming up · 12
+of 40 bars` and nothing is drawn. Every string is at most 30 characters.
+
+The table sits inside the price axis and SDK lines show no crosshair dots
+once the UX branch `ux/table-axis-inset` (main d065f7a) is in the build.
+
+## Controls
+
+Inputs: `Range length (bars)` 20 (10..50; the range needs twice this many bars
+before it can qualify), `Minimum bars inside` 6 (2..30), `Activity threshold
+(×)` 1.5 (1..3, step 0.1: one test passing gives a faded arrow, both a solid
+one). Advanced: `Width limit (× bar range)` 2 (1..4, step 0.5), `Failure window
+(bars)` 5, `Keep retired range (bars)` 40 (0..200), `Trend fast MA (bars)` 50
+(10..199), `Trend slow MA (bars)` 200 (20..400). Visibility: `Show break
+markers`, `Show ranges`. Every setting replays the retained bars when changed,
+the two visibility toggles included (markers and boxes need a replay to be
+rebuilt). No colour pickers in this version. Every key is in the schema, so
+presets work.
+
+## Alert conditions (rendered; no notification channel exists)
+
+**Break**: a close outside an established range with at least one activity
+test passed (`breakGrade ≥ 1`); arrow on the break bar. **Failed break**: a
+marked break whose price closed back inside within five bars; circle on that
+bar. Unconfirmed breaks (`breakGrade` 0) are table and box only. Measured on
+the controls at defaults: 0 markers per 1,000 bars on the ranging and the
+trending fixtures (see Evidence).
+
+## Historical Mode, replay and the retained history
+
+Policy `OHLCV_POLICY`: candles only, every archived instrument, equities
+included (no side is ever read; protocol check 9 is not applicable). Live and
+replay agree exactly for the same bars.
+
+What a reload can change (brief amendment 1): the chart keeps 10,080 one-minute
+bars, so a replay starts from the first retained bar. Every window is finite,
+but a range opened before the retained history exists live and not in the
+replay, and a continuously qualifying market reopens ranges at different
+phases. Outputs, events, markers and drawn ranges are therefore required equal
+from the first retained bar at which the live instance holds no active and no
+held range, once the 20 denominators and `trendSlow + 5` closes lie inside
+retention; before that bar the difference is the range phase, pinned in
+`tests/compressionBreakoutValidation.test.ts` on a 3,000-bar constant-width
+fixture rebuilt at offsets 0, 137 and 500 (the edges are identical on both
+sides, only age and expiry differ) and on a 1,400-bar coil rebuilt from index
+300 (both instances break at 740 with the same grade; their edges may differ
+before). After a reload a range older than the retained history is seen only
+from the first retained bar.
+
+## Limitations
+
+Descriptive, not predictive. Readiness: the range at 40 valid bars, grading
+at 20 valid bars, the trend at `trendSlow + 5`. The qualifier is a
+fixed-length range, not a volatility squeeze; the synthetic fixtures are
+monotone contractions that favour it, and the live run is where the two were
+compared (the squeeze qualifier stays a test-only variant; see the v2 clause
+in the brief). A failed break retires its range; a second break needs a fresh
+range. Ranges expire at 500 bars. Intraday equity ranges reset at the
+overnight gap (the session-open rule is deferred). Unit-free; no sided volume.
+
+## Evidence
+
+Design seeds 7..16 (both directions, 20 seed-directions; outcome fixtures 20
+each; controls 5 × 1,000 bars), reproduced through the runtime in
+`tests/compressionBreakoutBaseline.test.ts` and pinned there:
+
+| Def | Hits | Wrong dir | Early all (lead) | Early filtered | Level valid | Failure fixture | Hold fixture | Range control markers/1,000 | Trend control markers/1,000 |
+|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
+| Product (width ≤ 2×, min 6) | 20/20 | 0 | 4 (0) | 0 | 20/20 | 20/20 | 20/20 | 0.0 | 0.0 |
+| Plain frozen range (no width qualifier) | 20/20 | 0 | 42 (24) | 16 | 8/20 | 20/20 | 20/20 | 4.6 | 0.0 |
+| Squeeze-qualified variant (the squeeze qualifier on this machine, not the round-2 state machine with pause and release) | 20/20 | 0 | 28 (20) | 6 | 14/20 | 20/20 | 20/20 | 5.0 | 0.0 |
+| TTM release (timing and density only) | 18/20 | 0 | 32 (12) | 0 | n/a | none | none | 12.6 | 0.0 |
+| Bollinger crossing (timing and density only) | 20/20 | 0 | 118 (82) | 38 | n/a | none | none | 47.6 | 0.0 |
+
+Scoring: matched = a break event within two bars after the labelled break;
+hit = the first matched event has the labelled direction; wrong direction = a
+break event opposing the label inside that window; early = before the labelled
+break (lead-phase count in brackets); filtered = events whose bar passes the
+identical activity test (true range or volume at least 1.5× the preceding
+20-bar mean); level valid = the frozen edges lie inside the labelled compression
+range and the close is beyond the tested edge. The plain frozen-range baseline
+shares every rule but the width qualifier: its early events and its level
+validity are what the qualifier buys.

+| Bollinger crossing (timing and density only) | 10/10 | 0 | 56 (38) | 14 | n/a | 0 | 0 | none | none | 47.6 | 0.0 | 1 |
+
+The product meets the frozen criteria (hits ≥ 9 of 10 with level valid on every hit, filtered early ≤ 2, no wrong-direction event, failure and hold fixtures ≥ 4 of 5 per direction) with 10/10, 0, 0, 10/10 and 10/10.
+
+Performance (this container, Node 24, `tests/compressionBreakoutPerf.test.ts`):
+
+- Pooled replay of 10,080 one-minute bars, five timed runs after three warm-ups, `onBar` plus `readOutput` with the per-bar snapshot copy inside the timed region: p50 0.0042 ms, p99 0.0240 ms, max 9.761 ms (ceiling 0.05 ms at p99).
+- Real activation replay: p99 0.0205 ms, wall 80 ms (ceilings 0.5 ms and 2 s).
+- Forming bar with a range warmed on 10,080 bars, 500 revisions alternating a breaking and an inside close (250 marked): p99 0.0904 ms (ceiling 1 ms).
+- Rollover of 1,000 bars past retention: 10080 bars retained, outputs {"rangeHigh": 10080, "rangeLow": 10080}, 0 stale markers, 462 B per extra bar (within 3× the harness floor of about 370 B).
+
+Live observation on real Binance spot candles through the REST transport
+(`data-api.binance.vision`), density and replay only, no label; every def
+replayed over the same recordings (`pm/log/validation/compressionBreakout/live-run.md`
+holds the full event lists):

+1,819 bar frames (30 closes, 31 open times; 1,772 REPLACED_EDGE, 31 APPENDED,

+Over the seven retained days the product recorded 10 breaks (6 marked), 5
+failures and 5 held outside on BTC, and 10 breaks (8 marked), 5
+failures and 5 held outside on ETH. The
+squeeze qualifier on this machine fires six to seven times as often as the
+width qualifier on these bars; whether it catches coils the range misses is a
+question of level validity on labelled data, where the evaluation seeds showed
+none (see the v2 clause in the brief). The REBUILT mutations are closes that
+arrived after a newer forming bar on the polled transport; each one replays the
+retained bars, as in the chart.
+
+## Deviations from the brief
+
+- The comparator defs share the product's engine through a `qualifier` option
+  (`width`, `always`, `squeeze`), by design (code review ruling 4): the plain
+  baseline isolates the width qualifier under identical machinery, and the
+  independent validator's batch reference covers shared-defect risk. The
+  squeeze-qualified variant is the squeeze qualifier on this machine, not the
+  round-2 state machine with pause and release; nothing here validates that
+  round-2 machine.
+- The engine's rings are plain numeric rings rather than the keyed
+  `ObservationRing` of Relative Volume: corrections are by snapshot restore,
+  so no keyed update or eviction rollback is needed.
+- The UX pass (`pm/cycle2/ux-notes-compression-breakout.md`) is adopted in
+  full: full-colour table, single warm-up row, `Watching · width` row, row-4
+  formats, lo–hi edges, box alphas and borders, labels and hints.
