main
import type { BarFrame } from "../../../types/frames";

/**
 * Compression Range Breakout: the pure, incremental state machine.
 *
 * A committed `rangeLength`-bar high/low range qualifies when its width is at
 * most `widthMultiple` times the mean true range of the `rangeLength` valid
 * bars preceding that window. Its edges freeze the moment it opens. After
 * `minCompressionBars` committed bars whose close stayed inside, a close
 * outside is a break, graded by the break bar's true range and volume against
 * the preceding 20 valid observations. A close back inside (or beyond the
 * opposite edge) within `failBars` bars is a failure; otherwise the break is
 * "held outside". Failure, hold and a 500-bar lifetime retire the range; a
 * new one must qualify and establish again. No widening, no re-arm.
 *
 * Three named states (brief amendment 4): `P`, the committed state before the
 * tail bar, kept untouched for corrections of that tail; `W`, the working view
 * every evaluation reads (`P` plus the gap rule, never written); `C`, the
 * committed state after the tail's first closed dispatch, which seeds the next
 * `ts_open`. A forming revision evaluates against `P` only. A closed
 * re-dispatch of the tail recomputes `C` from `P`, so a cancelled break, a
 * removed failure, a changed grade and a validity flip in either field are all
 * one rule.
 *
 * Lifetime, the failure window and the hold countdown count committed
 * dispatched bars, invalid-price bars included (they skip every other commit
 * step); denominators count valid observations and carry no bar bound.
 *
 * No library stream drives an event: a never-closed bar left in a
 * `trading-signals` stream would change range creation. Every window here is a
 * finite ring of committed valid bars, so corrections and replays are exact.
 */

export const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
export const RANGE_LIFETIME = 500;
export const DENOMINATOR_LENGTH = 20;
/** The fast-MA history holds this many committed values: the slope compares the newest with the one five bars earlier. */
export const SLOPE_HISTORY = 6;
/** One active handle and seven held ones in the pool of eight; the oldest held range gives way to a new one. */
export const MAX_HELD_RANGES = 7;

export type Qualifier = "width" | "always" | "squeeze";

export interface CompressionBreakoutMathSettings {
  rangeLength: number;
  widthMultiple: number;
  minCompressionBars: number;
  confirmMultiple: number;
  failBars: number;
  holdBars: number;
  trendFast: number;
  trendSlow: number;
  /** How a range qualifies; the product uses `width`. `always` and `squeeze` exist for the test-only comparator defs. */
  qualifier?: Qualifier;
}

export type BreakGrade = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3;
export type GradeBasis = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3;
export type Direction = 1 | -1;

export interface BreakRecord {
  /** `committedIndex` of the break bar. */
  index: number;
  tsOpen: number;
  dir: Direction;
  grade: BreakGrade;
  basis: GradeBasis;
}

export interface ActiveRange {
  hi: number;
  lo: number;
  /** `committedIndex` of the bar whose commit opened the range. */
  openedAt: number;
  openedTs: number;
  /** Committed bars since opening whose close stayed inside (or pending bars after a break). */
  age: number;
  /** Width over reference at qualification; null for the `always` qualifier. */
  widthRatio: number | null;
  broken: BreakRecord | null;
  excursionHi: number;
  excursionLo: number;
}

export type Outcome = "failed" | "heldOutside" | "expired";

export interface HeldRange {
  hi: number;
  lo: number;
  openedTs: number;
  /** `ts_open` of the bar whose commit retired the range. */
  closedTs: number;
  outcome: Outcome;
  /** Committed bars the range is still drawn for; removed at the commit after it reaches 0. */
  remaining: number;
}

export type EventKind = "break" | "failed" | "heldOutside" | "expired";

export interface LastEvent {
  kind: EventKind;
  index: number;
  tsOpen: number;
  dir: Direction;
  grade: BreakGrade;
  basis: GradeBasis;
}

export interface CommittedState {
  /** Valid committed highs and lows, capacity `rangeLength`. */
  highs: number[];
  lows: number[];
  /** Valid committed true ranges, capacity `2 × rangeLength` (at least 20). */
  trs: number[];
  /** Valid committed volumes (price valid too), capacity 20. */
  volumes: number[];
  /** Valid committed closes, capacity `trendSlow` (at least 20). */
  closes: number[];
  /** Committed fast-MA values, capacity 6. */
  fastHistory: number[];
  prevClose: number | null;
  lastCommittedTsOpen: number | null;
  /** Committed dispatched bars so far; the next bar's index. */
  committedIndex: number;
  /** Committed valid-price bars so far: readiness counts these, the rings being capped. */
  validCount: number;
  /** An intraday gap seen by an invalid-price bar, kept until the next valid-price commit consumes it (code review finding 1). */
  pendingGapReset: boolean;
  active: ActiveRange | null;
  held: HeldRange[];
  lastEvent: LastEvent | null;
}

export type DecisionKind = "none" | "invalidPrice" | "break" | "abandoned" | "expired" | "failed" | "heldOutside" | "pending";

export interface Decision {
  kind: DecisionKind;
  dir: Direction | 0;
  grade: BreakGrade;
  basis: GradeBasis;
  rangePassed: boolean;
  volumePassed: boolean;
}

export type TrendStatus = "ready" | "warming" | "fastGeSlow";

/** Everything one dispatch of a bar yields, read against `W`; the output snapshot is taken here, before any retirement. */
export interface Reading {
  tsOpen: number;
  intervalMs: number;
  confirmed: boolean;
  priceValid: boolean;
  volumeValid: boolean;
  gap: boolean;
  trueRange: number | null;
  /** Valid committed bars before this one, capped at the range-creation readiness count. */
  warmupCount: number;
  warmupNeeded: number;
  rangeReady: boolean;
  /** The active range as tested (armed, forming or pending); null otherwise. */
  active: ActiveRange | null;
  rangeHigh: number | null;
  rangeLow: number | null;
  rangeAge: number | null;
  pending: 0 | 1;
  /** Committed bars since the break, this bar included, while pending; 0 otherwise. */
  pendingBars: number;
  /** Provisional qualification of the window ending at this bar (status row only). */
  qualified: boolean | null;
  widthRatio: number | null;
  trend: -1 | 0 | 1 | null;
  trendStatus: TrendStatus;
  closesCount: number;
  avgRange: number | null;
  avgVolume: number | null;
  gradeBasis: GradeBasis;
  decision: Decision;
  /** The held ranges to draw for this bar: `C.held` once closed, `P.held` while forming. */
  held: readonly HeldRange[];
  lastEvent: LastEvent | null;
}

export interface CompressionBreakoutEngine {
  readonly settings: CompressionBreakoutMathSettings;
  onBar(bar: BarFrame): Reading;
  readonly current: Reading | null;
  /** `P`: the committed state before the current tail. */
  readonly pre: CommittedState;
  /** `C`: the committed state after the tail's close (equals `P` while the tail is forming). */
  readonly committed: CommittedState;
}

export function normalizedSettings(settings: CompressionBreakoutMathSettings): Required<CompressionBreakoutMathSettings> {
  const int = (value: number, fallback: number, min: number, max: number): number =>
    Number.isFinite(value) ? Math.min(max, Math.max(min, Math.floor(value))) : fallback;
  const num = (value: number, fallback: number, min: number, max: number): number =>
    Number.isFinite(value) ? Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value)) : fallback;
  return {
    rangeLength: int(settings.rangeLength, 20, 10, 50),
    widthMultiple: num(settings.widthMultiple, 2, 1, 4),
    minCompressionBars: int(settings.minCompressionBars, 6, 2, 30),
    confirmMultiple: num(settings.confirmMultiple, 1.5, 1, 3),
    failBars: int(settings.failBars, 5, 1, 20),
    holdBars: int(settings.holdBars, 40, 0, 200),
    trendFast: int(settings.trendFast, 50, 10, 199),
    trendSlow: int(settings.trendSlow, 200, 20, 400),
    qualifier: settings.qualifier ?? "width",
  };
}

export function createCommittedState(): CommittedState {
  return {
    highs: [],
    lows: [],
    trs: [],
    volumes: [],
    closes: [],
    fastHistory: [],
    prevClose: null,
    lastCommittedTsOpen: null,
    committedIndex: 0,
    validCount: 0,
    pendingGapReset: false,
    active: null,
    held: [],
    lastEvent: null,
  };
}

function cloneState(state: CommittedState): CommittedState {
  return {
    highs: state.highs.slice(),
    lows: state.lows.slice(),
    trs: state.trs.slice(),
    volumes: state.volumes.slice(),
    closes: state.closes.slice(),
    fastHistory: state.fastHistory.slice(),
    prevClose: state.prevClose,
    lastCommittedTsOpen: state.lastCommittedTsOpen,
    committedIndex: state.committedIndex,
    validCount: state.validCount,
    pendingGapReset: state.pendingGapReset,
    active: state.active === null ? null : { ...state.active, broken: state.active.broken === null ? null : { ...state.active.broken } },
    held: state.held.map((range) => ({ ...range })),
    lastEvent: state.lastEvent === null ? null : { ...state.lastEvent },
  };
}

export function isPriceValid(bar: BarFrame): boolean {
  const { open, high, low, close } = bar;
  return (
    Number.isFinite(open) &&
    Number.isFinite(high) &&
    Number.isFinite(low) &&
    Number.isFinite(close) &&
    high >= low &&
    low <= close &&
    close <= high &&
    low <= open &&
    open <= high
  );
}

export function isVolumeValid(volume: unknown): volume is number {
  return typeof volume === "number" && Number.isFinite(volume) && volume >= 0;
}

/** Mean of the last `count` entries; null when fewer exist. */
export function tailMean(values: readonly number[], count: number): number | null {
  if (count <= 0 || values.length < count) return null;
  let sum = 0;
  for (let index = values.length - count; index < values.length; index += 1) sum += values[index];
  const mean = sum / count;
  return Number.isFinite(mean) ? mean : null;
}

/** Mean of `values[from, to)`; null when the slice is not fully present. */
function sliceMean(values: readonly number[], from: number, to: number): number | null {
  if (from < 0 || to > values.length || to <= from) return null;
  let sum = 0;
  for (let index = from; index < to; index += 1) sum += values[index];
  const mean = sum / (to - from);
  return Number.isFinite(mean) ? mean : null;
}

function pushCapped(values: number[], value: number, capacity: number): void {
  values.push(value);
  if (values.length > capacity) values.splice(0, values.length - capacity);
}

/**
 * True range against the previous committed close; `high − low` on the first
 * bar and on the first bar after a gap. Null when the subtraction overflows:
 * such a bar enters no range observation and its range test is not taken.
 */
export function trueRangeOf(bar: BarFrame, prevClose: number | null, gap: boolean): number | null {
  const span = bar.high - bar.low;
  const value = prevClose === null || gap ? span : Math.max(span, Math.abs(bar.high - prevClose), Math.abs(bar.low - prevClose));
  return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
}

/** A positive denominator from the last `DENOMINATOR_LENGTH` valid observations; null otherwise. */
export function denominatorOf(values: readonly number[]): number | null {
  const mean = tailMean(values, DENOMINATOR_LENGTH);
  return mean !== null && mean > 0 ? mean : null;
}

/** Population standard deviation of the last `count` values; null when fewer exist. */
function tailStdev(values: readonly number[], count: number, mean: number): number | null {
  if (values.length < count) return null;
  let sum = 0;
  for (let index = values.length - count; index < values.length; index += 1) sum += (values[index] - mean) ** 2;
  const stdev = Math.sqrt(sum / count);
  return Number.isFinite(stdev) ? stdev : null;
}

export interface Qualification {
  ready: boolean;
  qualified: boolean;
  widthRatio: number | null;
  hi: number;
  lo: number;
}

/**
 * The qualification of the window formed by the last `rangeLength` entries of
 * the given rings. On the committed rings this decides whether a range opens;
 * with the tail appended provisionally it feeds the status row only.
 */
export function qualificationOf(
  highs: readonly number[],
  lows: readonly number[],
  trs: readonly number[],
  closes: readonly number[],
  settings: Required<CompressionBreakoutMathSettings>,
): Qualification {
  const length = settings.rangeLength;
  const none: Qualification = { ready: false, qualified: false, widthRatio: null, hi: Number.NaN, lo: Number.NaN };
  if (highs.length < length || lows.length < length || trs.length < 2 * length) return none;
  let hi = -Infinity;
  let lo = Infinity;
  for (let index = highs.length - length; index < highs.length; index += 1) hi = Math.max(hi, highs[index]);
  for (let index = lows.length - length; index < lows.length; index += 1) lo = Math.min(lo, lows[index]);
  const width = hi - lo;
  if (!Number.isFinite(width)) return { ready: true, qualified: false, widthRatio: null, hi, lo };
  const reference = sliceMean(trs, trs.length - 2 * length, trs.length - length);
  const ratio = reference !== null && reference > 0 ? width / reference : null;
  const widthRatio = ratio !== null && Number.isFinite(ratio) ? ratio : null;
  if (settings.qualifier === "always") return { ready: true, qualified: true, widthRatio, hi, lo };
  if (settings.qualifier === "squeeze") {
    if (closes.length < length) return none;
    const mean = tailMean(closes, length);
    const stdev = mean === null ? null : tailStdev(closes, length, mean);
    const atr = tailMean(trs, length);
    const squeezed = mean !== null && stdev !== null && atr !== null && mean + 2 * stdev < mean + 1.5 * atr && mean - 2 * stdev > mean - 1.5 * atr;
    return { ready: true, qualified: squeezed, widthRatio, hi, lo };
  }
  return { ready: true, qualified: widthRatio !== null && widthRatio <= settings.widthMultiple, widthRatio, hi, lo };
}

export function createCompressionBreakoutEngine(input: CompressionBreakoutMathSettings): CompressionBreakoutEngine {
  const settings = normalizedSettings(input);
  const trCapacity = Math.max(2 * settings.rangeLength, DENOMINATOR_LENGTH);
  const closeCapacity = Math.max(settings.trendSlow, DENOMINATOR_LENGTH);
  const readinessCount = 2 * settings.rangeLength;
  let pre: CommittedState = createCommittedState();
  let committed: CommittedState = pre;
  let tailTsOpen: number | null = null;
  let current: Reading | null = null;

  function evaluate(P: CommittedState, bar: BarFrame): Reading {
    const priceValid = isPriceValid(bar);
    const volumeValid = isVolumeValid(bar.volume);
    const gapNow =
      P.lastCommittedTsOpen !== null &&
      Number.isFinite(bar.interval_ms) &&
      bar.interval_ms > 0 &&
      bar.interval_ms < DAY_MS &&
      bar.ts_open - P.lastCommittedTsOpen > bar.interval_ms;
    // A gap seen by an invalid-price bar is kept pending: that bar skips every commit step (amendment 5), and the
    // next valid-price bar performs the reset and takes `high − low` as its true range.
    const gap = gapNow || P.pendingGapReset;
    // W: P with the gap rule applied, never written.
    const active = gap && priceValid ? null : P.active;
    const trueRange = priceValid ? trueRangeOf(bar, P.prevClose, gap) : null;
    const avgRange = denominatorOf(P.trs);
    const avgVolume = denominatorOf(P.volumes);
    const rangeTestable = avgRange !== null && trueRange !== null;
    const volumeTestable = avgVolume !== null && volumeValid;
    const gradeBasis = ((rangeTestable ? 1 : 0) + (volumeTestable ? 2 : 0)) as GradeBasis;

    // Provisional qualification: the window of rangeLength − 1 committed bars plus the tail.
    let qualified: boolean | null = null;
    let widthRatio: number | null = null;
    if (priceValid && P.highs.length >= settings.rangeLength - 1 && P.trs.length >= 2 * settings.rangeLength - 1) {
      const provisional = qualificationOf(
        [...P.highs, bar.high],
        [...P.lows, bar.low],
        [...P.trs, trueRange as number],
        [...P.closes, bar.close],
        settings,
      );
      if (provisional.ready) {
        qualified = provisional.qualified;
        widthRatio = provisional.widthRatio;
      }
    }

    // Trend, from committed closes only; the tail's close is compared with them.
    let trendStatus: TrendStatus = "warming";
    let trend: -1 | 0 | 1 | null = null;
    if (settings.trendFast >= settings.trendSlow) {
      trendStatus = "fastGeSlow";
    } else if (P.validCount >= settings.trendSlow + 5 && P.closes.length >= settings.trendSlow && P.fastHistory.length >= SLOPE_HISTORY) {
      trendStatus = "ready";
      if (priceValid) {
        const slowMa = tailMean(P.closes, settings.trendSlow);
        const slope = P.fastHistory[P.fastHistory.length - 1] - P.fastHistory[0];
        if (slowMa !== null && Number.isFinite(slope)) trend = bar.close > slowMa && slope > 0 ? 1 : bar.close < slowMa && slope < 0 ? -1 : 0;
      }
    }

    // Decision, exactly one per bar, read against W.
    const index = P.committedIndex;
    let decision: Decision = { kind: "none", dir: 0, grade: 0, basis: gradeBasis, rangePassed: false, volumePassed: false };
    if (!priceValid) {
      decision = { ...decision, kind: "invalidPrice" };
    } else if (active !== null && active.broken !== null) {
      const inside = bar.close <= active.hi && bar.close >= active.lo;
      const opposite = active.broken.dir > 0 ? bar.close < active.lo : bar.close > active.hi;
      if (inside || opposite) decision = { ...decision, kind: "failed", dir: active.broken.dir, grade: active.broken.grade, basis: active.broken.basis };
      else if (index - active.broken.index >= settings.failBars)
        decision = { ...decision, kind: "heldOutside", dir: active.broken.dir, grade: active.broken.grade, basis: active.broken.basis };
      else decision = { ...decision, kind: "pending", dir: active.broken.dir, grade: active.broken.grade, basis: active.broken.basis };
    } else if (active !== null) {
      const dir: Direction | 0 = bar.close > active.hi ? 1 : bar.close < active.lo ? -1 : 0;
      if (dir !== 0) {
        if (active.age >= settings.minCompressionBars) {
          const rangePassed = rangeTestable && (trueRange as number) >= settings.confirmMultiple * (avgRange as number);
          const volumePassed = volumeTestable && bar.volume >= settings.confirmMultiple * (avgVolume as number);
          const grade: BreakGrade = rangePassed && volumePassed ? 3 : rangePassed ? 1 : volumePassed ? 2 : 0;
          decision = { kind: "break", dir, grade, basis: gradeBasis, rangePassed, volumePassed };
        } else {
          decision = { ...decision, kind: "abandoned", dir };
        }
      } else if (index - active.openedAt >= RANGE_LIFETIME) {
        decision = { ...decision, kind: "expired" };
      }
    }

    // Readiness counts committed valid-price bars plus this bar when its price is valid; the rings themselves are capped.
    const validThroughThisBar = P.validCount + (priceValid ? 1 : 0);
    return {
      tsOpen: bar.ts_open,
      intervalMs: bar.interval_ms,
      confirmed: bar.closed === true,
      priceValid,
      volumeValid,
      gap,
      trueRange,
      warmupCount: Math.min(validThroughThisBar, readinessCount),
      warmupNeeded: readinessCount,
      rangeReady: validThroughThisBar >= readinessCount,
      active,
      rangeHigh: active?.hi ?? null,
      rangeLow: active?.lo ?? null,
      rangeAge: active?.age ?? null,
      pending: active !== null && active.broken !== null ? 1 : 0,
      pendingBars: active !== null && active.broken !== null ? index - active.broken.index : 0,
      qualified,
      widthRatio,
      trend,
      trendStatus,
      closesCount: Math.min(P.validCount, settings.trendSlow + 5),
      avgRange,
      avgVolume,
      gradeBasis,
      decision,
      held: P.held,
      lastEvent: P.lastEvent,
    };
  }

  /** The commit of the tail's first closed dispatch: `C` from `P`, in the brief's order. */
  function commit(P: CommittedState, bar: BarFrame, reading: Reading): CommittedState {
    const C = cloneState(P);
    const index = P.committedIndex;
    // (a) held countdown: free the ones that reached zero at the previous commit, then count the rest down.
    C.held = C.held.filter((range) => range.remaining > 0).map((range) => ({ ...range, remaining: range.remaining - 1 }));
    if (!reading.priceValid) {
      // The clock advances below; a gap this bar saw waits for the next valid-price commit.
      C.pendingGapReset = P.pendingGapReset || reading.gap;
    } else {
      if (reading.gap) C.active = null;
      C.pendingGapReset = false;
      // (b) rings: only finite derived observations enter them.
      pushCapped(C.highs, bar.high, settings.rangeLength);
      pushCapped(C.lows, bar.low, settings.rangeLength);
      pushCapped(C.closes, bar.close, closeCapacity);
      if (reading.trueRange !== null) pushCapped(C.trs, reading.trueRange, trCapacity);
      if (reading.volumeValid) pushCapped(C.volumes, bar.volume, DENOMINATOR_LENGTH);
      const fastMa = tailMean(C.closes, settings.trendFast);
      if (fastMa !== null) pushCapped(C.fastHistory, fastMa, SLOPE_HISTORY);
      // (c) the decision
      const decision = reading.decision;
      const active = C.active;
      if (active !== null) {
        switch (decision.kind) {
          case "break":
            active.broken = { index, tsOpen: bar.ts_open, dir: decision.dir as Direction, grade: decision.grade, basis: decision.basis };
            active.excursionHi = bar.high;
            active.excursionLo = bar.low;
            C.lastEvent = { kind: "break", index, tsOpen: bar.ts_open, dir: decision.dir as Direction, grade: decision.grade, basis: decision.basis };
            break;
          case "failed":
          case "heldOutside":
          case "expired": {
            const dir = (active.broken?.dir ?? (bar.close >= active.hi ? 1 : -1)) as Direction;
            C.lastEvent = { kind: decision.kind, index, tsOpen: bar.ts_open, dir, grade: active.broken?.grade ?? 0, basis: active.broken?.basis ?? 0 };
            retire(C, active, decision.kind, bar.ts_open);
            C.active = null;
            break;
          }
          case "abandoned":
            C.active = null;
            break;
          case "pending":
            active.excursionHi = Math.max(active.excursionHi, bar.high);
            active.excursionLo = Math.min(active.excursionLo, bar.low);
            active.age += 1;
            break;
          case "none":
            active.age += 1;
            break;
          default:
            break;
        }
      }
      // (d) a new range opens only on a bar with no event, from the committed rings.
      if (C.active === null && decision.kind === "none") {
        const qualification = qualificationOf(C.highs, C.lows, C.trs, C.closes, settings);
        if (qualification.ready && qualification.qualified) {
          C.active = {
            hi: qualification.hi,
            lo: qualification.lo,
            openedAt: index,
            openedTs: bar.ts_open,
            age: 0,
            widthRatio: qualification.widthRatio,
            broken: null,
            excursionHi: qualification.hi,
            excursionLo: qualification.lo,
          };
        }
      }
      C.prevClose = bar.close;
      C.validCount += 1;
    }
    // (e) every committed dispatched bar advances the clock, valid or not.
    C.lastCommittedTsOpen = bar.ts_open;
    C.committedIndex = index + 1;
    return C;
  }

  function retire(C: CommittedState, range: ActiveRange, outcome: Outcome, closedTs: number): void {
    const held: HeldRange = { hi: range.hi, lo: range.lo, openedTs: range.openedTs, closedTs, outcome, remaining: settings.holdBars };
    C.held.push(held);
    // One active handle and seven held ones: the oldest held range gives way to a new one.
    if (C.held.length > MAX_HELD_RANGES) C.held.splice(0, C.held.length - MAX_HELD_RANGES);
  }

  function onBar(bar: BarFrame): Reading {
    if (tailTsOpen !== null && bar.ts_open < tailTsOpen) {
      // Older than the tail: the runtime replays those as a REBUILT; here it is ignored.
      return current ?? evaluate(pre, bar);
    }
    if (tailTsOpen === null || bar.ts_open > tailTsOpen) {
      // A new ts_open: C seeds P; a tail superseded without closing wrote nothing.
      pre = committed;
      tailTsOpen = bar.ts_open;
    }
    const reading = evaluate(pre, bar);
    if (bar.closed === true) {
      committed = commit(pre, bar, reading);
      reading.held = committed.held;
      reading.lastEvent = committed.lastEvent;
    } else {
      committed = pre;
    }
    current = reading;
    return reading;
  }

  return {
    settings,
    onBar,
    get current() {
      return current;
    },
    get pre() {
      return pre;
    },
    get committed() {
      return committed;
    },
  };
}

    14	/**
    15	 * Compression Breakout (20): a committed 20-bar high/low range that is
    16	 * narrower than twice the mean true range of the 20 bars before it, drawn as
    17	 * a neutral purple box with frozen edges; a break is a close outside it after
    18	 * six bars inside, graded by the break bar's true range and volume against the
    19	 * preceding 20 bars (arrow solid when both pass, faded when one does); a close
    20	 * back inside within five bars is a failed break (circle); otherwise the break
    21	 * is "held outside 5 bars". Trend (MA 50/200) colours the border and one table
    22	 * row only. Candle-only, `OHLCV_POLICY`.

    80	/** `#rgb` or `#rrggbb` with an alpha; any other colour string is returned unchanged. */
    81	export function withAlpha(color: string, alpha: number): string {
    82	  const hex = /^#([0-9a-f]{3}|[0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(color.trim());
    83	  if (hex === null) return color;
    84	  let digits = hex[1];
    85	  if (digits.length === 3) digits = digits.split("").map((d) => d + d).join("");
    86	  const r = Number.parseInt(digits.slice(0, 2), 16);
    87	  const g = Number.parseInt(digits.slice(2, 4), 16);
    88	  const b = Number.parseInt(digits.slice(4, 6), 16);
    89	  return `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${alpha})`;
    90	}
    91	
    92	export function trendColor(trend: -1 | 0 | 1 | null): string {
    93	  if (trend === 1) return CB_BULL_COLOR;
    94	  if (trend === -1) return CB_BEAR_COLOR;
    95	  return CB_RANGE_COLOR;
    96	}
    97	
    98	/** Decimal places a price carries, from its shortest round-trip text; at most eight. */
    99	export function decimalsOf(value: number): number {
   100	  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return 0;
   101	  const text = String(value);
   102	  const exponent = text.indexOf("e");
   103	  if (exponent !== -1) return Math.min(8, Math.max(0, -Number.parseInt(text.slice(exponent + 1), 10)));
   104	  const point = text.indexOf(".");
   105	  return point === -1 ? 0 : Math.min(8, text.length - point - 1);
   106	}
   107	
   108	function formatAt(value: number, decimals: number): string {
   109	  return value.toFixed(decimals);
   110	}
   111	
   112	/** Six significant digits with trailing zeros removed, for a row that would not fit. */
   113	function formatSignificant(value: number): string {
   114	  const text = Number(value.toPrecision(6)).toString();
   115	  return text.includes("e") ? value.toPrecision(6) : text;
   116	}
   117	
   118	/**
   119	 * `Range lo–hi` at the instrument's precision with no thousands separators; if
   120	 * the row would exceed 30 characters, both edges round to six significant
   121	 * digits (brief ruling 8).
   122	 */
   123	export function rangeRowText(hi: number, lo: number): string {
   124	  const decimals = Math.max(decimalsOf(hi), decimalsOf(lo));
   125	  const exact = `Range ${formatAt(lo, decimals)}–${formatAt(hi, decimals)}`;
   126	  if (exact.length <= CB_TABLE_MAX_CHARS) return exact;
   127	  return `Range ${formatSignificant(lo)}–${formatSignificant(hi)}`;
   128	}
   129	
   130	function arrow(dir: number): string {
   131	  return dir > 0 ? "↑" : "↓";
   132	}
   133	
   134	/** The test-basis wording of a break: what passed, and what could not be tested. */
   135	export function breakBasisText(grade: number, basis: number): string {
   136	  const rangeTestable = (basis & 1) === 1;
   137	  const volumeTestable = (basis & 2) === 2;
   138	  const rangePassed = grade === 1 || grade === 3;
   139	  const volumePassed = grade === 2 || grade === 3;
   140	  if (rangeTestable && volumeTestable) {
   141	    if (rangePassed && volumePassed) return "range+vol";
   142	    if (rangePassed) return "range only";
   143	    if (volumePassed) return "vol only";
   144	    return "unconfirmed";
   145	  }
   146	  if (rangeTestable) return rangePassed ? "range, vol n/a" : "vol n/a";
   147	  if (volumeTestable) return volumePassed ? "vol, range n/a" : "range n/a";
   148	  return "no baseline";
   149	}
   150	
   151	function timeText(tsOpen: number, intervalMs: number): string {
   152	  const date = new Date(tsOpen);
   153	  if (intervalMs >= 86_400_000) return `${date.getUTCDate()} ${["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"][date.getUTCMonth()]}`;
   154	  return `${String(date.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(date.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
   155	}
   156	
   157	export interface TableRow {
   158	  text: string;
   159	  color: string;
   160	}
   161	
   162	/** Row 3 of the table: the state, first match wins (brief rule 12 and amendments 1, 5 and 7). */
   163	export function stateText(reading: Reading, settings: CompressionBreakoutSettings): string {
   164	  const d = reading.decision;
   165	  if (d.kind === "invalidPrice") return "No usable price";
   166	  if (reading.gap) return "Gap · range reset";
   167	  if (d.kind === "failed") return "Failed break";
   168	  if (d.kind === "heldOutside") return `Held outside · ${settings.failBars} bars`;
   169	  if (d.kind === "expired") return `Range expired · ${RANGE_LIFETIME} bars`;
   170	  if (d.kind === "break") return `Break ${arrow(d.dir)} · ${breakBasisText(d.grade, d.basis)}`;
   171	  const active = reading.active;
   172	  if (d.kind === "pending") return `Holding · ${Math.min(settings.failBars, reading.pendingBars)} of ${settings.failBars} bars`;
   173	  if (active !== null) {
   174	    if (active.age >= settings.minCompressionBars) {
   175	      const ratio = active.widthRatio === null ? "" : ` · width ${active.widthRatio.toFixed(1)}×`;
   176	      return `Range · ${active.age} bars${ratio}`;
   177	    }
   178	    return `Range forming · ${active.age} of ${settings.minCompressionBars}`;
   179	  }
   180	  // No range: how far the rolling window is from the width limit, so the row carries a reason.
   181	  return reading.widthRatio === null ? "Watching" : `Watching · width ${reading.widthRatio.toFixed(1)}×`;
   182	}
   183	
   184	export function trendText(reading: Reading, settings: CompressionBreakoutSettings): string {
   185	  const pair = `MA ${settings.trendFast}/${settings.trendSlow}`;
   186	  if (reading.trendStatus === "fastGeSlow") return "Trend off · fast ≥ slow";
   187	  if (reading.trendStatus === "warming") return `Trend warming · ${reading.closesCount} of ${settings.trendSlow + 5}`;
   188	  if (reading.trend === 1) return `Trend ↑ · ${pair}`;
   189	  if (reading.trend === -1) return `Trend ↓ · ${pair}`;
   190	  return `Trend flat · ${pair}`;
   191	}
   192	
   193	export function lastEventText(reading: Reading, settings: CompressionBreakoutSettings): string {
   194	  const event = reading.lastEvent;
   195	  if (event === null) return CB_UNAVAILABLE;
   196	  const when = timeText(event.tsOpen, reading.intervalMs);
   197	  switch (event.kind) {
   198	    case "break":
   199	      return `Last ${arrow(event.dir)} ${when} · ${breakBasisText(event.grade, event.basis)}`;
   200	    case "failed":
   201	      return `Last failed ${arrow(event.dir)} ${when}`;
   202	    case "heldOutside":
   203	      return `Last held ${arrow(event.dir)} ${when} · ${settings.failBars} bars`;
   204	    default:
   205	      return `Last expired ${when}`;
   206	  }
   207	}
   208	
   209	/**
   210	 * The table rows: during warm-up only `[{ text }]` (one row is written, so one
   211	 * row shows); afterwards the four rows at full colour. Provisional styling
   212	 * stays on markers and on the edge lines' last point only: the table is
   213	 * flushed after every forming revision, so a dimmed forming row would read
   214	 * as a permanently dimmed table (UX finding 1).
   215	 */
   216	export function tableRows(reading: Reading | null, settings: CompressionBreakoutSettings): TableRow[] {
   217	  const neutral = CB_TABLE_TEXT_COLOR;
   218	  if (reading === null) return [{ text: `Warming up · 0 of ${2 * settings.rangeLength} bars`, color: neutral }];
   219	  if (!reading.rangeReady) return [{ text: `Warming up · ${reading.warmupCount} of ${reading.warmupNeeded} bars`, color: neutral }];
   220	  const row1 = reading.active !== null ? rangeRowText(reading.active.hi, reading.active.lo) : "No range";
   221	  const d = reading.decision;
   222	  const stateColor =
   223	    d.kind === "break" ? (d.dir > 0 ? CB_BULL_COLOR : CB_BEAR_COLOR) : d.kind === "failed" ? CB_WARN_COLOR : neutral;
   224	  return [
   225	    { text: row1, color: neutral },
   226	    { text: trendText(reading, settings), color: trendColor(reading.trend) },
   227	    { text: stateText(reading, settings), color: stateColor },
   228	    { text: lastEventText(reading, settings), color: neutral },
   229	  ];
   230	}
   231	
   232	function hideBox(box: BoxHandle): void {
   233	  box.setBackgroundColor(TRANSPARENT);
   234	  box.setBorderColor(TRANSPARENT);
   235	}
   236	
   237	function drawActive(box: BoxHandle, reading: Reading, settings: CompressionBreakoutSettings): void {
   238	  const active = reading.active;
   239	  if (active === null) {
   240	    hideBox(box);
   241	    return;
   242	  }
   243	  const established = active.age >= settings.minCompressionBars;
   244	  box.setLeftTop(active.openedTs, active.hi);
   245	  box.setRightBottom(reading.tsOpen, active.lo);
   246	  box.setBackgroundColor(withAlpha(CB_RANGE_COLOR, established ? CB_FILL_ESTABLISHED_ALPHA : CB_FILL_FORMING_ALPHA));
   247	  // On the outcome bar the range already wears its outcome: amber when the break failed, purple when it held or expired.
   248	  const kind = reading.decision.kind;
   249	  const border = kind === "failed" ? CB_WARN_COLOR : kind === "heldOutside" || kind === "expired" ? CB_RANGE_COLOR : established ? trendColor(reading.trend) : withAlpha(CB_RANGE_COLOR, CB_FORMING_BORDER_ALPHA);
   250	  box.setBorderColor(border);
   251	  box.setBorderWidth(1);
   252	}
   253	
   254	function drawHeld(box: BoxHandle, held: HeldRange | undefined, tsOpen: number): void {
   255	  // A range retired on this very bar is still shown through the active snapshot; it joins the held drawing from the next bar.
   256	  if (held === undefined || held.closedTs >= tsOpen) {
   257	    hideBox(box);
   258	    return;
   259	  }
   260	  box.setLeftTop(held.openedTs, held.hi);
   261	  box.setRightBottom(held.closedTs, held.lo);
   262	  box.setBackgroundColor(withAlpha(CB_RANGE_COLOR, CB_FILL_HELD_ALPHA));
   263	  box.setBorderColor(withAlpha(held.outcome === "failed" ? CB_WARN_COLOR : CB_RANGE_COLOR, CB_HELD_BORDER_ALPHA));
   264	  box.setBorderWidth(1);
   265	}
   266	
   267	function plotMarkers(ctx: IndicatorBarCtx<CompressionBreakoutState, CompressionBreakoutSettings>, reading: Reading): void {
   268	  const d = reading.decision;
   269	  if (d.kind === "break" && d.grade >= 1) {
   270	    const base = d.dir > 0 ? CB_BULL_COLOR : CB_BEAR_COLOR;
   271	    const alpha = (d.grade === 3 ? 1 : CB_FADED_MARKER_ALPHA) * (reading.confirmed ? 1 : CB_PROVISIONAL_ALPHA);
   272	    ctx.markers.plotshape({
   273	      shape: d.dir > 0 ? "arrowUp" : "arrowDown",
   274	      position: d.dir > 0 ? "belowBar" : "aboveBar",
   275	      color: alpha === 1 ? base : withAlpha(base, alpha),
   276	      size: 1,
   277	    });
   278	  } else if (d.kind === "failed" && d.grade >= 1) {
   279	    ctx.markers.plotshape({
   280	      shape: "circle",
   281	      position: "inBar",
   282	      color: reading.confirmed ? CB_WARN_COLOR : withAlpha(CB_WARN_COLOR, CB_PROVISIONAL_ALPHA),
   283	      size: 1,
   284	    });
   285	  }
   286	}
   287	
   288	/**
   289	 * The edge lines' per-bar colour. The chart paints each segment with the
   290	 * colour of the point it starts from, so the last point of a range (the bar
   291	 * whose decision retires it) is painted transparent: the segment that would
   292	 * otherwise join this range's edge to the next range's edge never appears.
   293	 */
   294	export function edgeLineColor(state: CompressionBreakoutState): string {
   295	  const reading = state.reading;
   296	  if (reading === null) return CB_RANGE_COLOR;
   297	  const kind = reading.decision.kind;
   298	  if (kind === "failed" || kind === "heldOutside" || kind === "expired" || kind === "abandoned") return CB_EDGE_JOIN_COLOR;
   299	  return CB_RANGE_COLOR;
   300	}
   301	
   302	const LINES: readonly LineOutputSpec<CompressionBreakoutState>[] = [
   303	  { key: "rangeHigh", lineWidth: 1, color: edgeLineColor, recolor: (current) => current ?? CB_RANGE_COLOR },
   304	  { key: "rangeLow", lineWidth: 1, color: edgeLineColor, recolor: (current) => current ?? CB_RANGE_COLOR },
   305	];
   306	
   307	const SCHEMA: readonly SettingsFieldSchema<CompressionBreakoutSettings>[] = [
   308	  { kind: "numeric", key: "rangeLength", label: "Range length (bars)", min: 10, max: 50, step: 1, affects: "recompute" },
   309	  { kind: "staticHint", text: "The range needs twice this many bars before it can qualify. Replays every bar." },
   310	  { kind: "numeric", key: "minCompressionBars", label: "Minimum bars inside", min: 2, max: 30, step: 1, affects: "recompute" },
   311	  { kind: "staticHint", text: "Closed bars inside the range before a close outside counts as a break. Replays every bar." },
   312	  { kind: "numeric", key: "confirmMultiple", label: "Activity threshold (×)", min: 1, max: 3, step: 0.1, affects: "recompute" },
   313	  { kind: "staticHint", text: "The break bar's range and volume versus the prior 20 bars: one passing gives a faded arrow, both a solid one. Replays every bar." },
   314	  { kind: "numeric", key: "widthMultiple", label: "Width limit (× bar range)", min: 1, max: 4, step: 0.5, affects: "recompute", tab: "advanced" },
   315	  { kind: "staticHint", text: "A range qualifies when narrower than this many times the mean bar range before it. Replays every bar.", tab: "advanced" },
   316	  { kind: "numeric", key: "failBars", label: "Failure window (bars)", min: 1, max: 20, step: 1, affects: "recompute", tab: "advanced" },
   317	  { kind: "numeric", key: "holdBars", label: "Keep retired range (bars)", min: 0, max: 200, step: 1, affects: "recompute", tab: "advanced" },
   318	  { kind: "staticHint", text: "Bars a failed or held range stays drawn. Replays every bar.", tab: "advanced" },
   319	  { kind: "numeric", key: "trendFast", label: "Trend fast MA (bars)", min: 10, max: 199, step: 1, affects: "recompute", tab: "advanced" },
   320	  { kind: "numeric", key: "trendSlow", label: "Trend slow MA (bars)", min: 20, max: 400, step: 1, affects: "recompute", tab: "advanced" },
   321	  { kind: "boolean", key: "showMarkers", label: "Show break markers", affects: "recompute", tab: "visibility" },
   322	  { kind: "boolean", key: "showRanges", label: "Show ranges", affects: "recompute", tab: "visibility" },
   323	];
   324	
   325	export const compressionBreakoutIndicator: RuntimeIndicatorDef<CompressionBreakoutState, CompressionBreakoutSettings> = {
   326	  kind: "runtime",
   327	  id: "compressionBreakout",
   328	  label: "Compression Breakout (20)",
   329	  category: "technical",
   330	  defaultSettings: COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
   331	  settingsSchema: SCHEMA,
   332	  lines: LINES,
   333	  placement: { kind: "overlay" },
   334	  maxReplayBars: 10_080,
   335	  createState: (settings) => ({
   336	    engine: createCompressionBreakoutEngine(settings),
   337	    table: draw.table(),
   338	    boxes: Array.from({ length: CB_BOX_POOL }, () =>
   339	      draw.box({ time1: 0, price1: 0, time2: 0, price2: 0, borderColor: TRANSPARENT, backgroundColor: TRANSPARENT, borderWidth: 1 }),
   340	    ),
   341	    reading: null,
   342	  }),
   343	  onBar: (ctx) => {
   344	    const { bar, state, settings } = ctx;
   345	    const reading = state.engine.onBar(bar);
   346	    state.reading = reading;
   347	    if (settings.showMarkers) plotMarkers(ctx, reading);
   348	    if (ctx.drawNow === false) return;
   349	    if (settings.showRanges) {
   350	      drawActive(state.boxes[0], reading, settings);
   351	      for (let slot = 0; slot < MAX_HELD_RANGES; slot += 1) drawHeld(state.boxes[slot + 1], reading.held[slot], reading.tsOpen);
   352	    } else {
   353	      for (const box of state.boxes) hideBox(box);
   354	    }
   355	    const rows = tableRows(reading, settings);
   356	    for (let row = 0; row < rows.length; row += 1) state.table.cell(row, 0, { text: rows[row].text, textColor: rows[row].color });
   357	  },
   358	  readOutput: (state) => {
   359	    const reading = state.reading;
   360	    if (reading === null) {
   361	      return { rangeHigh: null, rangeLow: null, activeHigh: null, activeLow: null, heldCount: 0, widthRatio: null, qualified: null, trend: null, rangeAge: null, pending: 0, breakDir: 0, breakGrade: 0, gradeBasis: 0, failed: 0, heldOutside: 0, expired: 0 };
   362	    }
   363	    const d = reading.decision;
   364	    const isBreak = d.kind === "break";
   365	    // The brief's edge keys carry the snapshot whenever a range exists, forming included; `edgeLineColor` paints a
   366	    // range's last point transparent so successive ranges never join into a slope that never existed.
   367	    return {
   368	      rangeHigh: reading.rangeHigh,
   369	      rangeLow: reading.rangeLow,
   370	      // The same snapshot under the names the harness tests used, and the held count: the replay policy's "quiet bar"
   371	      // has neither an active nor a held range.
   372	      activeHigh: reading.rangeHigh,
   373	      activeLow: reading.rangeLow,
   374	      heldCount: reading.held.length,
   375	      widthRatio: reading.widthRatio,
   376	      qualified: reading.qualified === null ? null : reading.qualified ? 1 : 0,
   377	      trend: reading.trend,
   378	      rangeAge: reading.rangeAge,
   379	      pending: reading.pending,
   380	      breakDir: isBreak ? d.dir : 0,
   381	      breakGrade: isBreak ? d.grade : 0,
   382	      gradeBasis: isBreak ? d.basis : 0,
   383	      failed: d.kind === "failed" ? 1 : 0,
   384	      heldOutside: d.kind === "heldOutside" ? 1 : 0,
   385	      expired: d.kind === "expired" ? 1 : 0,
   386	    };
   387	  },
   388	};

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
import { BINANCE_PERP, DXFEED_EQUITY, barFrame } from "../../../chart/validation";
import {
  DAY_MS,
  DENOMINATOR_LENGTH,
  MAX_HELD_RANGES,
  RANGE_LIFETIME,
  createCompressionBreakoutEngine,
  isPriceValid,
  qualificationOf,
  normalizedSettings,
  trueRangeOf,
  type CommittedState,
  type CompressionBreakoutMathSettings,
  type Reading,
} from "../compressionBreakoutMath";

/**
 * Success criterion 1, hand-computed. The base pattern alternates a down bar
 * (open 60050, close 59950) and an up bar (open 59950, close 60050) with wicks
 * of 5, so every bar's true range is 110, every 20-bar window spans
 * 59945..60055 (width 110), the reference mean true range is 110 and the
 * width ratio is exactly 1. Volume is 100 on every base bar. At the defaults
 * the range opens at the commit of index 39 with age 0, bars 40..45 inside
 * make it eligible, and index 46 is the first bar that can break (amendment 2).
 */

const T0 = 1_780_000_000_000;
const MINUTE = 60_000;
const SETTINGS: CompressionBreakoutMathSettings = {
  rangeLength: 20,
  widthMultiple: 2,
  minCompressionBars: 6,
  confirmMultiple: 1.5,
  failBars: 5,
  holdBars: 40,
  trendFast: 50,
  trendSlow: 200,
};

interface Spec {
  open: number;
  high: number;
  low: number;
  close: number;
  volume: number;
}

function baseSpec(index: number): Spec {
  const up = index % 2 === 1;
  return up ? { open: 59_950, high: 60_055, low: 59_945, close: 60_050, volume: 100 } : { open: 60_050, high: 60_055, low: 59_945, close: 59_950, volume: 100 };
}

function bar(index: number, spec: Spec, overrides: Partial<BarFrame> = {}, intervalMs = MINUTE): BarFrame {
  const frame = barFrame(T0 + index * intervalMs, { ...spec, tradeCount: 10 }, BINANCE_PERP, intervalMs);
  return { ...frame, ...overrides } as BarFrame;
}

/** `count` base bars with `specAt` overriding any index. */
function series(count: number, specAt: (index: number) => Spec | null = () => null, intervalMs = MINUTE): BarFrame[] {
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => bar(index, specAt(index) ?? baseSpec(index), {}, intervalMs));
}

function run(bars: readonly BarFrame[], settings: CompressionBreakoutMathSettings = SETTINGS): { readings: Reading[]; committed: CommittedState } {
  const engine = createCompressionBreakoutEngine(settings);
  const readings = bars.map((frame) => engine.onBar(frame));
  return { readings, committed: engine.committed };
}

/** Index 46 is even, so its previous committed close is 60050: true range = max(260, 255, 5) = 260 ≥ 1.5 × 110. */
const BREAK_UP: Spec = { open: 60_050, high: 60_305, low: 60_045, close: 60_300, volume: 200 };

describe("compressionBreakoutMath -- validity and true range", () => {
  it("price validity needs finite values with low ≤ open, close ≤ high", () => {
    expect(isPriceValid(bar(0, baseSpec(0)))).toBe(true);
    expect(isPriceValid(bar(0, baseSpec(0), { high: 59_000 }))).toBe(false);
    expect(isPriceValid(bar(0, baseSpec(0), { open: 70_000 }))).toBe(false);
    expect(isPriceValid(bar(0, baseSpec(0), { close: 50_000 }))).toBe(false);
    expect(isPriceValid(bar(0, baseSpec(0), { low: Number.NaN }))).toBe(false);
  });

  it("true range uses the previous committed close, and high − low on the first bar and after a gap", () => {
    const up = bar(1, baseSpec(1));
    expect(trueRangeOf(up, null, false)).toBe(110);
    expect(trueRangeOf(up, 59_950, false)).toBe(110);
    expect(trueRangeOf(up, 50_000, false)).toBe(10_055);
    expect(trueRangeOf(up, 50_000, true)).toBe(110);
  });

  it("settings are brought into their schema ranges", () => {
    const n = normalizedSettings({ ...SETTINGS, rangeLength: 5, widthMultiple: 9, trendFast: 0, trendSlow: 1_000 });
    expect(n.rangeLength).toBe(10);
    expect(n.widthMultiple).toBe(4);
    expect(n.trendFast).toBe(10);
    expect(n.trendSlow).toBe(400);
    expect(n.qualifier).toBe("width");
  });
});

describe("compressionBreakoutMath -- establishment timeline (amendment 2)", () => {
  it("readiness, qualification and the opening commit at index 39 with age 0; eligible at 46", () => {
    const { readings, committed } = run(series(46));
    for (let index = 0; index < 39; index += 1) {
      expect(readings[index].rangeReady).toBe(false);
      expect(readings[index].qualified).toBeNull();
      expect(readings[index].rangeHigh).toBeNull();
    }
    expect(readings[39].rangeReady).toBe(true);
    expect(readings[39].warmupCount).toBe(40);
    expect(readings[39].qualified).toBe(true);
    expect(readings[39].widthRatio).toBe(1);
    expect(readings[39].rangeHigh).toBeNull(); // the range opens at this bar's commit, after the snapshot
    expect(readings[40].rangeHigh).toBe(60_055);
    expect(readings[40].rangeLow).toBe(59_945);
  it("a close back inside the frozen edges is a failure; the edges are still reported on that bar; the range is held", () => {
    const { readings, committed } = run(breakThenInside(60_000));
    expect(readings[47].pending).toBe(1);
    expect(readings[47].pendingBars).toBe(1);
    expect(readings[47].decision).toMatchObject({ kind: "failed", dir: 1, grade: 3 });
    expect(readings[47].rangeHigh).toBe(60_055);
    expect(committed.active).toBeNull();
    expect(committed.held).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(committed.held[0]).toMatchObject({ hi: 60_055, lo: 59_945, outcome: "failed", remaining: 40 });
    expect(committed.lastEvent).toMatchObject({ kind: "failed", index: 47 });
  });

  it("a close beyond the opposite edge is a failure too (it passed back through the range)", () => {
    const { readings } = run(breakThenInside(59_900));
    expect(readings[47].decision.kind).toBe("failed");
  });

  it("five bars outside, the last included: held outside at index 51 with the edges still reported; nothing from 52", () => {
    const outside = { open: 60_300, high: 60_315, low: 60_295, close: 60_310, volume: 100 };
    const { readings, committed } = run(series(53, (index) => (index === 46 ? BREAK_UP : index > 46 ? outside : null)));
    for (let index = 47; index <= 50; index += 1) {
      expect(readings[index].decision.kind).toBe("pending");
      expect(readings[index].pending).toBe(1);
    }
    expect(readings[51].decision).toMatchObject({ kind: "heldOutside", dir: 1, grade: 3 });
    expect(readings[51].pending).toBe(1);
    expect(readings[51].rangeHigh).toBe(60_055);
    expect(readings[52].pending).toBe(0);
    expect(readings[52].rangeHigh).toBeNull();
    expect(committed.held[0].outcome).toBe("heldOutside");
    expect(committed.lastEvent).toMatchObject({ kind: "heldOutside", index: 51 });
  });

  it("an invalid-price bar inside the window counts for the clock but decides nothing: still held outside at 51", () => {
    const outside = { open: 60_300, high: 60_315, low: 60_295, close: 60_310, volume: 100 };
    const bars = series(53, (index) => (index === 46 ? BREAK_UP : index > 46 ? outside : null));
    bars[48] = { ...bars[48], high: 1 } as BarFrame;
    const { readings } = run(bars);
    expect(readings[48].decision.kind).toBe("invalidPrice");
    expect(readings[48].pending).toBe(1);
    expect(readings[51].decision.kind).toBe("heldOutside");
    expect(run(bars).committed.validCount).toBe(52);
    expect(run(bars).committed.committedIndex).toBe(53);
  });

  it("the held countdown: drawn for holdBars bars after the outcome bar and freed at the commit after; holdBars 0 keeps nothing", () => {
    const long = series(100, (index) => (index === 46 ? BREAK_UP : index === 47 ? { open: 60_300, high: 60_305, low: 59_995, close: 60_000, volume: 100 } : null));
    const { readings } = run(long);
    expect(readings[47].held).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(readings[47].held[0].remaining).toBe(40);
    expect(readings[87].held).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(readings[87].held[0].remaining).toBe(0);
    expect(readings[88].held).toHaveLength(0);
    const none = run(long, { ...SETTINGS, holdBars: 0 }).readings;
    expect(none[47].held).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(none[47].held[0].remaining).toBe(0);
    expect(none[48].held).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("the pool keeps the seven newest held ranges: the oldest gives way to the eighth", () => {
    // Cycles of a small break (close one above the edge) and a return inside, so every window qualifies again at once.
    const bars: BarFrame[] = [];
    let hi = 60_055;
    let index = 0;
    const push = (spec: Spec) => {
      bars.push(bar(index, spec));
      index += 1;
    };
    for (let base = 0; base < 40; base += 1) push(baseSpec(base));
    for (let cycle = 0; cycle < 9; cycle += 1) {
      // The range re-opens at the commit of the bar after a failure, which does not count as inside: one more base bar per later cycle.
      for (let inside = 0; inside < (cycle === 0 ? 6 : 7); inside += 1) push(baseSpec(index));
      const close = hi + 1;
      push({ open: 60_050, high: close + 5, low: 60_045, close, volume: 200 });
      push({ open: close, high: close + 5, low: 59_995, close: 60_000, volume: 100 });
      hi = close + 5;
    }
    const { readings, committed } = run(bars, { ...SETTINGS, holdBars: 200 });
    const failures = readings.filter((reading) => reading.decision.kind === "failed");
    expect(failures).toHaveLength(9);
    expect(committed.held).toHaveLength(MAX_HELD_RANGES);
    expect(committed.held[0].closedTs).toBe(failures[2].tsOpen);
    expect(committed.held[MAX_HELD_RANGES - 1].closedTs).toBe(failures[8].tsOpen);
  });
});

import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
import type { CompressionBreakoutLabels, Outputs } from "../../../chart/validation";

/**
 * The scoring of the brief and amendment 8, identical for every def:
 * matched = a break event inside `[breakoutIndex, breakoutIndex + 2]`; hit =
 * the first matched event carries the labelled direction; wrong-direction = a
 * break event opposing the label inside that window; early = before the
 * window (split at `compressionStart`); unmatched = after it; missed = no
 * matched event; level valid = on a hit, the frozen edges lie inside the
 * labelled compression range and the close is beyond the tested edge.
 * Filtering keeps only events whose bar passes the identical activity test
 * (true range or volume at or above 1.5 times the mean of the preceding 20
 * bars), computed here from the bars alone, independently of any def.
 */

export interface BreakEvent {
  index: number;
  dir: number;
  grade: number | null;
}

export function breakEventsOf(outputs: Outputs): BreakEvent[] {
  const events: BreakEvent[] = [];
  const dirs = outputs.breakDir ?? [];
  const grades = outputs.breakGrade;
  dirs.forEach((dir, index) => {
    if (dir !== null && dir !== 0) events.push({ index, dir, grade: grades === undefined ? null : (grades[index] ?? null) });
  });
  return events;
}

/** The identical activity filter: true range or volume at least 1.5× the mean of the preceding 20 bars. */
export function activityFlags(bars: readonly BarFrame[], multiple = 1.5): boolean[] {
  const flags: boolean[] = [];
  const trs: number[] = [];
  const vols: number[] = [];
  let prevClose: number | null = null;
  for (const bar of bars) {
    const tr = prevClose === null ? bar.high - bar.low : Math.max(bar.high - bar.low, Math.abs(bar.high - prevClose), Math.abs(bar.low - prevClose));
    const mean = (values: number[]): number | null => (values.length < 20 ? null : values.slice(-20).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / 20);
    const avgTr = mean(trs);
    const avgVol = mean(vols);
    const rangePass = avgTr !== null && avgTr > 0 && tr >= multiple * avgTr;
    const volumePass = avgVol !== null && avgVol > 0 && bar.volume >= multiple * avgVol;
    flags.push(rangePass || volumePass);
    trs.push(tr);
    vols.push(bar.volume);
    prevClose = bar.close;
  }
  return flags;
}

export interface Score {
  hit: 0 | 1;
  wrongDirection: number;
  matched: number;
  early: number;
  earlyLead: number;
  earlyCompression: number;
  unmatched: number;
  missed: 0 | 1;
  /** 1 when the hit's edges and close satisfy the level rule; 0 when a hit fails it; null without a hit or without edges. */
  levelValid: 0 | 1 | null;
}

export function scoreBreakout(
  bars: readonly BarFrame[],
  outputs: Outputs,
  labels: CompressionBreakoutLabels,
  filter: readonly boolean[] | null = null,
  tolerance = 2,
): Score {
  const events = breakEventsOf(outputs).filter((event) => filter === null || filter[event.index]);
  const b = labels.breakoutIndex;
  const score: Score = { hit: 0, wrongDirection: 0, matched: 0, early: 0, earlyLead: 0, earlyCompression: 0, unmatched: 0, missed: 0, levelValid: null };
  let first: BreakEvent | null = null;
  for (const event of events) {
    if (event.index >= b && event.index <= b + tolerance) {
      score.matched += 1;
      if (event.dir !== labels.direction) score.wrongDirection += 1;
      if (first === null) first = event;
    } else if (event.index < b) {
      score.early += 1;
      if (event.index < labels.compressionStart) score.earlyLead += 1;
      else score.earlyCompression += 1;
    } else {
      score.unmatched += 1;
    }
  }
  if (first === null) {
    score.missed = 1;
    return score;
  }
  if (first.dir === labels.direction) {
    score.hit = 1;
    const hi = outputs.activeHigh?.[first.index];
    const lo = outputs.activeLow?.[first.index];
    if (hi !== undefined && lo !== undefined && hi !== null && lo !== null) {
      const close = bars[first.index].close;
      const beyond = labels.direction > 0 ? close > hi : close < lo;
      score.levelValid = hi <= labels.compressionHigh + 1e-9 && lo >= labels.compressionLow - 1e-9 && beyond ? 1 : 0;
    }
  }
  return score;
}

export function addScores(total: Record<string, number>, score: Score): void {
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(score)) {
    if (value === null) continue;
    total[key] = (total[key] ?? 0) + value;
  }
  if (score.levelValid !== null) total.levelChecked = (total.levelChecked ?? 0) + 1;
}

/** The labelled outcome: a break at `breakoutIndex` and the pulse at `outcomeIndex`. */
export function outcomeHit(outputs: Outputs, breakoutIndex: number, outcomeIndex: number, key: "failed" | "heldOutside"): boolean {
  const broke = (outputs.breakDir?.[breakoutIndex] ?? 0) !== 0;
  const outcome = (outputs[key]?.[outcomeIndex] ?? 0) === 1;
  return broke && outcome;
}

export default defineConfig(({ mode }) => configForEnv(proxyEnvForMode(mode)))
import type { LineOutputSpec, RuntimeIndicatorDef } from "../../../chart/indicatorTypes";
import { ta } from "../../../chart/ta";
import type { Stream } from "../../../chart/ta/stream";
import { CB_BEAR_COLOR, CB_BULL_COLOR, CB_WARN_COLOR, withAlpha, type CompressionBreakoutSettings } from "../compressionBreakout";
import { createCompressionBreakoutEngine, type CompressionBreakoutEngine, type Qualifier, type Reading } from "../compressionBreakoutMath";

/**
 * The test-only comparator defs of the brief, each with the same output seams
 * the scoring reads (`breakDir`, `breakGrade`, `failed`, `heldOutside`,
 * `activeHigh`, `activeLow`) declared as lines so the harness records them.
 *
 * - `plainFrozenRangeBaseline`: the product's establishment, grading and
 *   outcome rules with no width qualifier at all (every ready window opens a
 *   range), amendment 8's "does compression selection discriminate" baseline.
 * - `squeezeQualifiedVariant`: the squeeze qualifier (Bollinger inside a
 *   Keltner-style channel over the committed window) on this machine, not the
 *   round-2 state machine with its pause and release rules: the comparison
 *   isolates the qualifier the v2 clause asks about (code review ruling 4).
 * - `ttmReleaseComparator`: the library's TTM Squeeze releasing after at least
 *   six squeezed bars; direction from the momentum sign. Timing and density
 *   only: it has no level and no outcome.
 * - `bollingerCrossingComparator`: the first close outside a Bollinger band
 *   after an inside close. Timing and density only.
 */

const SEAMS = ["breakDir", "breakGrade", "gradeBasis", "failed", "heldOutside", "activeHigh", "activeLow"] as const;

interface EngineState {
  engine: CompressionBreakoutEngine;
  reading: Reading | null;
}

function engineDef(id: string, qualifier: Qualifier): RuntimeIndicatorDef<EngineState, CompressionBreakoutSettings> {
  return {
    kind: "runtime",
    id,
    label: id,
    category: "technical",
    defaultSettings: {
      rangeLength: 20,
      minCompressionBars: 6,
      confirmMultiple: 1.5,
      widthMultiple: 2,
      failBars: 5,
      holdBars: 40,
      trendFast: 50,
      trendSlow: 200,
      showMarkers: true,
      showRanges: true,
    },
    lines: SEAMS.map((key) => ({ key })) as readonly LineOutputSpec<EngineState>[],
    placement: { kind: "overlay" },
    createState: (settings) => ({ engine: createCompressionBreakoutEngine({ ...settings, qualifier }), reading: null }),
    onBar: (ctx) => {
      const reading = ctx.state.engine.onBar(ctx.bar);
      ctx.state.reading = reading;
      const d = reading.decision;
      if (d.kind === "break" && d.grade >= 1) {
        const base = d.dir > 0 ? CB_BULL_COLOR : CB_BEAR_COLOR;
        ctx.markers.plotshape({ shape: d.dir > 0 ? "arrowUp" : "arrowDown", position: d.dir > 0 ? "belowBar" : "aboveBar", color: d.grade === 3 ? base : withAlpha(base, 0.65), size: 1 });
      } else if (d.kind === "failed" && d.grade >= 1) {
        ctx.markers.plotshape({ shape: "circle", position: "inBar", color: CB_WARN_COLOR, size: 1 });
      }
    },
    readOutput: (state) => {
      const r = state.reading;
      if (r === null) return { breakDir: 0, breakGrade: 0, gradeBasis: 0, failed: 0, heldOutside: 0, activeHigh: null, activeLow: null };
      const isBreak = r.decision.kind === "break";
      return {
        breakDir: isBreak ? r.decision.dir : 0,
        breakGrade: isBreak ? r.decision.grade : 0,
        gradeBasis: isBreak ? r.decision.basis : 0,
        failed: r.decision.kind === "failed" ? 1 : 0,
        heldOutside: r.decision.kind === "heldOutside" ? 1 : 0,
        activeHigh: r.rangeHigh,
        activeLow: r.rangeLow,
      };
    },
  };
}

export const plainFrozenRangeBaseline = engineDef("plainFrozenRangeBaseline", "always");
export const squeezeQualifiedVariant = engineDef("squeezeQualifiedVariant", "squeeze");

interface TtmState {
  stream: Stream<{ high: number; low: number; close: number }, { isSqueezed: boolean; momentum: number }>;
  committedRun: number;
  pendingSqueezed: boolean | null;
  breakDir: number;
}

/** TTM Squeeze release: the first bar `isSqueezed` is false after at least `minSqueezedBars` consecutive true bars. */
export const ttmReleaseComparator: RuntimeIndicatorDef<TtmState, { minSqueezedBars: number }> = {
  kind: "runtime",
  id: "ttmReleaseComparator",
  label: "ttmReleaseComparator",
  category: "technical",
  defaultSettings: { minSqueezedBars: 6 },
  lines: [{ key: "breakDir" }],
  placement: { kind: "overlay" },
  createState: () => ({ stream: ta.ttmSqueeze(20, 2, 20, 1.5), committedRun: 0, pendingSqueezed: null, breakDir: 0 }),
  onBar: (ctx) => {
    const { bar, state, settings, replace } = ctx;
    if (!replace && state.pendingSqueezed !== null) state.committedRun = state.pendingSqueezed ? state.committedRun + 1 : 0;
    const result = state.stream.push({ high: bar.high, low: bar.low, close: bar.close }, replace);
    state.pendingSqueezed = result === null ? null : result.isSqueezed;
    state.breakDir = 0;
    if (result !== null && !result.isSqueezed && state.committedRun >= settings.minSqueezedBars) {
      state.breakDir = Math.sign(result.momentum);
      if (state.breakDir !== 0) ctx.markers.plotshape({ shape: state.breakDir > 0 ? "arrowUp" : "arrowDown", position: state.breakDir > 0 ? "belowBar" : "aboveBar", color: state.breakDir > 0 ? CB_BULL_COLOR : CB_BEAR_COLOR, size: 1 });

import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { BarFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";
import { resampleBars } from "../../../chart/resampleBars";
import { diffMarkers, diffOutputs, markerBudgetOf, openLiveSession, replayCapture, type Outputs } from "../../../chart/validation";
import { COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, compressionBreakoutIndicator } from "../compressionBreakout";
import { bollingerCrossingComparator, plainFrozenRangeBaseline, squeezeQualifiedVariant, ttmReleaseComparator } from "./comparators";
import { compressionBreakoutSeamDef } from "./seamDef";

afterEach(() => {
  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
});

/**
 * The live observation run of the brief: real Binance spot candles recorded
 * from the REST transport (snapshot of 10,080 one-minute bars, then every bar
 * frame for thirty minutes) are driven through the real runtime the way the
 * chart receives them, then compared with a clean replay of the retained
 * series. Density, honesty and replay only: there is no label. Set
 * CB_LIVE_DIR to the directory holding `btc-usdt.json` and `eth-usdt.json`
 * (the recorder is `record-live.cjs`); the report goes to CB_LIVE_OUT.
 */

interface Recording {
  symbol: string;
  startedAt: string;
  endedAt: string;
  snapshotBars: BarFrame[];
  barFrames: (BarFrame & { receivedAt: number })[];
  counts: Record<string, number>;
}

const DIR = process.env.CB_LIVE_DIR ?? "";
const FILES = ["btc-usdt.json", "eth-usdt.json"].map((name) => join(DIR, name));
const available = DIR !== "" && FILES.every((file) => existsSync(file));

function eventsOf(outputs: Outputs, bars: readonly BarFrame[]): string[] {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  bars.forEach((bar, index) => {
    const when = new Date(bar.ts_open).toISOString().slice(5, 16).replace("T", " ");
    const dir = outputs.breakDir?.[index] ?? 0;
    if (dir !== 0) lines.push(outputs.breakGrade === undefined ? `${when} event ${dir > 0 ? "↑" : "↓"}` : `${when} break ${dir > 0 ? "↑" : "↓"} grade ${outputs.breakGrade[index]} basis ${outputs.gradeBasis?.[index]}`);
    if ((outputs.failed?.[index] ?? 0) === 1) lines.push(`${when} failed`);
    if ((outputs.heldOutside?.[index] ?? 0) === 1) lines.push(`${when} held outside`);
    if ((outputs.expired?.[index] ?? 0) === 1) lines.push(`${when} expired`);
  });
  return lines;
}

describe.skipIf(!available)("compressionBreakout -- live observation on BTC-USDT and ETH-USDT spot candles (REST transport)", () => {
  const report: string[] = ["# Compression Breakout live observation run", ""];

  it("live equals the replay of the retained series, nothing faults, and the marker density is under 40 per 1,000 on 1m and on resampled 5m", () => {
    for (const file of FILES) {
      const recording = JSON.parse(readFileSync(file, "utf8")) as Recording;
      const frames = recording.barFrames.map(({ receivedAt: _receivedAt, ...bar }) => bar as BarFrame);
      expect(recording.snapshotBars.length).toBeGreaterThan(1_000);
      // The chart's path: the snapshot is replayed on activation, then every bar frame is dispatched as it arrived.
      const session = openLiveSession(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, {}, recording.snapshotBars);
      let live;
      let retained: BarFrame[];
      let mutations: string[];
      try {
        for (const frame of frames) session.dispatch({ bar: frame });
        live = session.capture();
        retained = [...session.bars];
        mutations = [...session.mutations];
      } finally {
        session.close();
      }
      expect(live.faulted).toBe(false);
      const replay = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, retained, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
      // Amendment 1: the rollover dropped the oldest bars the live instance saw, so the replay starts cold; equality is
      // required from the first retained bar at which the live instance holds no active and no held range once the
      // denominators and the trend window lie inside retention. Differences before it are reported, not judged.
      const warm = Math.max(2 * COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS.rangeLength, 20, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS.trendSlow + 5);
      let quiet = -1;
      for (let index = warm; index < retained.length; index += 1) {
        if (live.outputs.activeHigh[index] === null && live.outputs.heldCount[index] === 0) {
          quiet = index;
          break;
        }
      }
      const allDiffs = diffOutputs(live.outputs, replay.outputs);
      const preQuiet = allDiffs.filter((diff) => quiet === -1 || diff.index < quiet).length;
      const outputDiffs = quiet === -1 ? allDiffs : allDiffs.filter((diff) => diff.index >= quiet);
      const firstDiff = outputDiffs.length === 0 ? -1 : Math.min(...outputDiffs.map((diff) => diff.index));
      const productLive = (() => {
        const s = openLiveSession(compressionBreakoutIndicator, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, {}, recording.snapshotBars);
        try {
          for (const frame of frames) s.dispatch({ bar: frame });
          return s.capture();
        } finally {
          s.close();
        }
      })();
      const productReplay = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutIndicator, retained, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
      const confirmedLive = productLive.markers.filter((call) => !call.color.startsWith("rgba("));
      const confirmedReplay = productReplay.markers.filter((call) => !call.color.startsWith("rgba("));
      const quietTime = quiet === -1 ? -Infinity : retained[quiet].ts_open;
      const markerDiff = diffMarkers(confirmedLive.filter((call) => call.time >= quietTime), confirmedReplay.filter((call) => call.time >= quietTime));
      const budget1m = markerBudgetOf(confirmedReplay, retained.length, productReplay.faulted);
      const fiveMinute = resampleBars(retained, 5 * 60_000);
      const replay5m = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutIndicator, fiveMinute, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
      const budget5m = markerBudgetOf(replay5m.markers.filter((call) => !call.color.startsWith("rgba(")), fiveMinute.length, replay5m.faulted);
      const plain = replayCapture(plainFrozenRangeBaseline, retained, plainFrozenRangeBaseline.defaultSettings);
      // Ruling 5: every comparator over the same recording, offline.
      const comparators = [
        { name: "Plain frozen range (no width qualifier)", capture: plain },
        { name: "Squeeze-qualified variant (the squeeze qualifier on this machine)", capture: replayCapture(squeezeQualifiedVariant, retained, squeezeQualifiedVariant.defaultSettings) },
        { name: "TTM release (timing and density only)", capture: replayCapture(ttmReleaseComparator, retained, ttmReleaseComparator.defaultSettings) },
        { name: "Bollinger crossing (timing and density only)", capture: replayCapture(bollingerCrossingComparator, retained, bollingerCrossingComparator.defaultSettings) },
      ];
      const comparators5m = [
        { name: "Plain frozen range (no width qualifier)", capture: replayCapture(plainFrozenRangeBaseline, fiveMinute, plainFrozenRangeBaseline.defaultSettings) },
        { name: "Squeeze-qualified variant (the squeeze qualifier on this machine)", capture: replayCapture(squeezeQualifiedVariant, fiveMinute, squeezeQualifiedVariant.defaultSettings) },
        { name: "TTM release (timing and density only)", capture: replayCapture(ttmReleaseComparator, fiveMinute, ttmReleaseComparator.defaultSettings) },
        { name: "Bollinger crossing (timing and density only)", capture: replayCapture(bollingerCrossingComparator, fiveMinute, bollingerCrossingComparator.defaultSettings) },
      ];
      const rangesOpened = replay.outputs.rangeAge.filter((age) => age === 0).length;
      const closes = frames.filter((frame) => frame.closed).length;
      report.push(
        `## ${recording.symbol}`,
        "",
        `Recorded ${recording.startedAt} to ${recording.endedAt}: snapshot ${recording.snapshotBars.length} bars, ${frames.length} bar frames (${closes} closes, ${new Set(frames.map((frame) => frame.ts_open)).size} distinct open times), mutations ${JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries([...new Set(mutations)].map((kind) => [kind, mutations.filter((m) => m === kind).length])))}.`,
        "",
        `Live versus replay of the ${retained.length} retained bars (the rollover dropped ${recording.snapshotBars.length + closes - retained.length} older bars the live instance had seen): first quiet bar at retained index ${quiet} (${quiet === -1 ? "none" : new Date(retained[quiet].ts_open).toISOString()}), ${retained.length - Math.max(quiet, 0)} bars compared after it with ${outputDiffs.length} seam disagreements${firstDiff >= 0 ? ` (first at index ${firstDiff})` : ""}; ${preQuiet} seam cells differ before the quiet bar (the replay starts cold); confirmed markers after the quiet bar live-only ${markerDiff.leftOnly.length}, replay-only ${markerDiff.rightOnly.length}; later snapshot re-sends during the run: ${(recording as Recording & { laterSnapshots?: unknown[] }).laterSnapshots?.length ?? 0}; faulted: ${live.faulted || replay.faulted}.`,
        "",
        `Ranges opened over the retained series: ${rangesOpened}. Markers: ${budget1m.total} on 1m (${budget1m.per1000Bars.toFixed(1)} per 1,000, max ${budget1m.maxPerBar} per bar); ${budget5m.total} on resampled 5m over ${fiveMinute.length} bars (${budget5m.per1000Bars.toFixed(1)} per 1,000).`,
        "",
        "Density of every def on the same bars (confirmed markers; the two timing comparators mark every event):",
        "",
        "| Def | Events on 1m | Markers on 1m | per 1,000 | Markers on 5m | per 1,000 |",
        "|---|---|---|---|---|---|",
      );
      const productEvents = eventsOf(replay.outputs, retained);
      report.push(`| Product (width ≤ 2×, min 6) | ${productEvents.length} | ${budget1m.total} | ${budget1m.per1000Bars.toFixed(1)} | ${budget5m.total} | ${budget5m.per1000Bars.toFixed(1)} |`);
      const eventLists: { name: string; events: string[] }[] = [{ name: "Product", events: productEvents }];
      comparators.forEach((comparator, position) => {
        const events = eventsOf(comparator.capture.outputs, retained);
        const b1 = markerBudgetOf(comparator.capture.markers, retained.length, comparator.capture.faulted);
        const b5 = markerBudgetOf(comparators5m[position].capture.markers, fiveMinute.length, comparators5m[position].capture.faulted);
        expect(comparator.capture.faulted).toBe(false);
        report.push(`| ${comparator.name} | ${events.length} | ${b1.total} | ${b1.per1000Bars.toFixed(1)} | ${b5.total} | ${b5.per1000Bars.toFixed(1)} |`);
        eventLists.push({ name: comparator.name, events });
      });
      report.push("");
      for (const list of eventLists) {
        report.push(`### ${recording.symbol}: ${list.name} (${list.events.length} events)`, "");
        if (list.events.length === 0) report.push("(none)");
        for (const line of list.events) report.push(`- ${line}`);
        report.push("");
      }
      expect(quiet).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
      expect(retained.length - quiet).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(outputDiffs).toEqual([]);
      expect(markerDiff.leftOnly).toEqual([]);
      expect(markerDiff.rightOnly).toEqual([]);
      expect(budget1m.per1000Bars).toBeLessThanOrEqual(40);
      expect(budget5m.per1000Bars).toBeLessThanOrEqual(40);
      expect(budget1m.maxPerBar).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
    }
    const text = report.join("\n");
    console.log(text);
    if (process.env.CB_LIVE_OUT) {
      mkdirSync(dirname(process.env.CB_LIVE_OUT), { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(process.env.CB_LIVE_OUT, `${text}\n`);
    }
  }, 600_000);
});

import { create, remove, tablesForOwners, update } from "./registry";

export type TablePosition = "top-right" | "bottom-right";

export interface TableOptions {
  position?: TablePosition;
}

export interface TableCellOptions {
  text: string;
  bgColor?: string;
  textColor?: string;
}

export interface TableCell {
  text: string;
  bgColor: string;
  textColor: string;
}

export interface TableData {
  position: TablePosition;
  pendingGrid: Record<string, TableCell>;
  committedGrid: Record<string, TableCell>;
}

export interface TableHandle {
  cell(row: number, col: number, options: TableCellOptions): void;
  delete(): void;
}

export const DEFAULT_TABLE_CELL_BG = "transparent";
export const DEFAULT_TABLE_CELL_TEXT_COLOR = "#d1d4dc";

function cellKey(row: number, col: number): string {
  return `${row},${col}`;
}

export function createTable(ownerId: string, options: TableOptions = {}): TableHandle {
  const handleId = create<TableData>(ownerId, "table", { position: options.position ?? "top-right", pendingGrid: {}, committedGrid: {} });

  return {
    cell(row, col, options) {
      update<TableData>(handleId, (data) => ({
        ...data,
        pendingGrid: {
          ...data.pendingGrid,
          [cellKey(row, col)]: {
            text: options.text,
            bgColor: options.bgColor ?? DEFAULT_TABLE_CELL_BG,
            textColor: options.textColor ?? DEFAULT_TABLE_CELL_TEXT_COLOR,
          },
        },
      }));
    },
    delete() {
      remove(handleId);
    },
  };
}

/**
 * Flips every table object owned by `ownerId` from its buffered pending grid
 * to the committed grid actually read for rendering -- called once per bar
 * transition (never per `.cell()` call) so a table's on-screen content only
 * ever changes once per bar, matching plotshape/plotchar's own discipline.
 */
export function flushTableCells(ownerId: string): void {
  for (const record of tablesForOwners([ownerId])) {
    update<TableData>(record.handleId, (data) => ({ ...data, committedGrid: data.pendingGrid }));
  }
}
