/**
 * Success criterion 3 and protocol check 6, measured the frozen way: three
 * untimed warm-up replays, five timed replays of the first 10,080 crypto
 * bars, per-bar `onBar` plus `readOutput` pooled (the per-bar snapshot copy
 * is inside the timed region), p99 by nearest rank. The forming-bar probe
 * warms a range on 10,080 constant-width bars, then revises the next bar 500
 * times alternating a breaking close and an inside close, so the break
 * marker appears and disappears on every revision. Set CB_PERF_OUT=<file>
 * to keep the JSON.
 */

const P99_CEILING_MS = 0.05;
const FORMING_REVISIONS = 500;

describe("compressionBreakout -- performance", () => {
  const { bars, labels } = relativeVolumeCryptoFixture();
  const timing = bars.slice(labels.timingIndices.first, labels.timingIndices.last + 1);

  it("pooled onBar plus readOutput p99 under 0.05 ms over 10,080 bars; forming revisions under 1 ms; bounded state after the rollover", () => {
    expect(timing).toHaveLength(10_080);
    const pooled = probeReplayPooled(compressionBreakoutIndicator, timing, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, { warmupRuns: 3, timedRuns: 5 });
    const replay = probeReplay(compressionBreakoutIndicator, timing, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    const rollover = probeRollover(compressionBreakoutIndicator, timing, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, { extraBars: 1_000 });

    const warm = constantWidthBars({ count: 10_081 }).bars;
    const { def: timed, samples } = timedDef(compressionBreakoutIndicator);
    const session = openLiveSession(timed, COMPRESSION_BREAKOUT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, {}, warm.slice(0, 10_080));
    const next = warm[10_080];
    let marked = 0;
    let formingWallMs = 0;
    try {
      samples.length = 0;
      const started = performance.now();
      for (let revision = 0; revision < FORMING_REVISIONS; revision += 1) {
        const breaking = revision % 2 === 0;
        const frame = { ...next, open: 60_050, high: breaking ? 60_305 : 60_055, low: 60_045, close: breaking ? 60_300 : 60_050, volume: 200, closed: revision === FORMING_REVISIONS - 1 } as BarFrame;
        session.dispatch({ bar: frame });
        if (session.runtime.getMarkers(timed.id)!.some((call) => call.time === next.ts_open)) marked += 1;
      }
      formingWallMs = performance.now() - started;
      expect(session.capture().faulted).toBe(false);
    } finally {
      session.close();
    }
    const forming = percentilesOf(samples);
    expect(forming.count).toBe(FORMING_REVISIONS);
    expect(marked).toBe(FORMING_REVISIONS / 2);

    const lines = [
      formatPooledReplayProbe(pooled),
      formatReplayProbe(replay),
      `compressionBreakout forming bar, range warmed on 10,080 bars, ${FORMING_REVISIONS} revisions crossing the edge each time (${marked} marked): onBar p50 ${forming.p50.toFixed(4)} ms p99 ${forming.p99.toFixed(4)} ms max ${forming.max.toFixed(3)} ms; wall ${formingWallMs.toFixed(0)} ms`,
      formatRolloverProbe(rollover),
    ];
    console.log(lines.join("\n"));
    if (process.env.CB_PERF_OUT) {
      mkdirSync(dirname(process.env.CB_PERF_OUT), { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(process.env.CB_PERF_OUT, `${JSON.stringify({ node: process.version, pooled, replay, rollover, forming: { revisions: FORMING_REVISIONS, marked, onBar: forming, wallMs: formingWallMs } }, null, 2)}\n`);
    }
    expect(pooled.faulted).toBe(false);
    expect(pooled.pooled.count).toBe(10_080 * 5);
    expect(pooled.pooled.p99).toBeLessThan(P99_CEILING_MS);
    expect(replay.faulted).toBe(false);
    expect(replay.onBar.p99).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5);
    expect(replay.wallMs).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2_000);
    expect(forming.p99).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(rollover.faulted).toBe(false);
    expect(rollover.retainedBars).toBe(MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS);
    expect(rollover.outputLengths).toEqual({ rangeHigh: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS, rangeLow: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS });
    expect(rollover.markersBeforeRetention).toBe(0);
  }, 600_000);
});

describe("compressionBreakout -- replay policy (amendment 1)", () => {
  function compareFromQuiet(bars: readonly BarFrame[], offset: number, label: string): number {
    const live = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars, SETTINGS);
    const rebuilt = replayCapture(compressionBreakoutSeamDef, bars.slice(offset), SETTINGS);
    const quiet = quietBarIndex(live.outputs, offset, SETTINGS);
    expect(quiet).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
    let compared = 0;
    for (let index = quiet; index < bars.length; index += 1) {
      for (const key of SEAM_KEYS) expect(rebuilt.outputs[key][index - offset]).toBe(live.outputs[key][index]);
      compared += 1;
    }
    const liveMarkers = live.markers.filter((call) => call.time >= bars[quiet].ts_open).map((call) => JSON.stringify(call));
    const rebuiltMarkers = rebuilt.markers.filter((call) => call.time >= bars[quiet].ts_open).map((call) => JSON.stringify(call));
    expect(rebuiltMarkers).toEqual(liveMarkers);

    60	
    61	export function tableFor(seeds: readonly number[]): Record<string, Row> {
    62	  const rows: Record<string, Row> = {};
    63	  for (const { name, def, settings, outcomes } of DEFS) {
    64	    const total: Record<string, number> = {};
    65	    const filtered: Record<string, number> = {};
    66	    let failures = 0;
    67	    let holds = 0;
    68	    for (const direction of [1, -1] as const) {
    69	      for (const seed of seeds) {
    70	        const fixture = compressionBreakoutBars({ seed, direction });
    71	        const flags = activityFlags(fixture.bars);
    72	        const outputs = replayCapture(def, fixture.bars, settings).outputs;
    73	        addScores(total, scoreBreakout(fixture.bars, outputs, fixture.labels));
    74	        addScores(filtered, scoreBreakout(fixture.bars, outputs, fixture.labels, flags));
    75	        if (outcomes) {
    76	          const failed = compressionFailedBreakBars({ seed, direction });
    77	          if (outcomeHit(replayCapture(def, failed.bars, settings).outputs, failed.labels.breakoutIndex, failed.labels.failIndex, "failed")) failures += 1;
    78	          const held = compressionContinuationBars({ seed, direction });
    79	          if (outcomeHit(replayCapture(def, held.bars, settings).outputs, held.labels.breakoutIndex, held.labels.heldIndex, "heldOutside")) holds += 1;
    80	        }
    81	      }
    82	    }
    83	    let rangeMarkers = 0;
    84	    let trendMarkers = 0;
    85	    let maxPerBar = 0;
    86	    for (let seed = 7; seed <= 11; seed += 1) {
    87	      const range = countMarkers(def, rangeBars({ seed, count: 1_000 }).bars, settings);
    88	      const trend = countMarkers(def, trendBars({ seed, count: 1_000 }).bars, settings);
    89	      rangeMarkers += range.total;
    90	      trendMarkers += trend.total;
    91	      maxPerBar = Math.max(maxPerBar, range.maxPerBar, trend.maxPerBar);
    92	    }
    93	    rows[name] = {
    94	      hits: total.hit ?? 0,
    95	      wrongDirection: total.wrongDirection ?? 0,
    96	      earlyAll: total.early ?? 0,
    97	      earlyLead: total.earlyLead ?? 0,
    98	      earlyFiltered: filtered.early ?? 0,
    99	      unmatched: total.unmatched ?? 0,
   100	      missed: total.missed ?? 0,
   101	      levelValid: total.levelValid ?? 0,
   102	      levelChecked: total.levelChecked ?? 0,
   103	      failures,
   104	      holds,
   105	      rangeMarkersPer1000: rangeMarkers / 5,
   106	      trendMarkersPer1000: trendMarkers / 5,
   107	      maxPerBar,
   108	    };
   109	  }
   110	  return rows;
   111	}
   112	
   113	export function formatTable(title: string, rows: Record<string, Row>, runs: number): string {
   114	  const lines = [
   115	    `${title} (${runs} seed-directions; outcome fixtures ${runs} each)`,
   116	    "| Def | Hits | Wrong dir | Early all (lead) | Early filtered | Level valid | Missed | Unmatched | Failure fixture | Hold fixture | Range control markers/1,000 | Trend control markers/1,000 | Max per bar |",
   117	    "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|",
   118	  ];
   119	  for (const [name, r] of Object.entries(rows)) {
   120	    const level = r.levelChecked === 0 ? "n/a" : `${r.levelValid}/${r.levelChecked}`;
   121	    const outcomes = name.includes("timing") ? ["none", "none"] : [`${r.failures}/${runs}`, `${r.holds}/${runs}`];
   122	    lines.push(`| ${name} | ${r.hits}/${runs} | ${r.wrongDirection} | ${r.earlyAll} (${r.earlyLead}) | ${r.earlyFiltered} | ${level} | ${r.missed} | ${r.unmatched} | ${outcomes[0]} | ${outcomes[1]} | ${r.rangeMarkersPer1000.toFixed(1)} | ${r.trendMarkersPer1000.toFixed(1)} | ${r.maxPerBar} |`);
   123	  }
   124	  lines.push("", "The squeeze-qualified variant is the squeeze qualifier on this machine, not the round-2 state machine with pause and release; the plain frozen range shares the machine by design, to isolate the width qualifier.");
   125	  return lines.join("\n");
